     1→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type {
     3→  ContextPacket,
     4→  DecisionContext,
     5→  TaskContext,
     6→  QuestionContext,
     7→  ObservationContext,
     8→  TaskScopeContext,
     9→  RecentChangeContext,
    10→} from "./types";
    11→
    12→type ProjectRow = {
    13→  id: RecordId<"project", string>;
    14→  name: string;
    15→  status: string;
    16→  description?: string;
    17→};
    18→
    19→type DecisionRow = {
    20→  id: RecordId<"decision", string>;
    21→  summary: string;
    22→  status: string;
    23→  rationale?: string;
    24→  decided_at?: Date | string;
    25→  category?: string;
    26→  priority?: string;
    27→};
    28→
    29→type TaskRow = {
    30→  id: RecordId<"task", string>;
    31→  title: string;
    32→  status: string;
    33→  priority?: string;
    34→  category?: string;
    35→  description?: string;
    36→};
    37→
    38→type QuestionRow = {
    39→  id: RecordId<"question", string>;
    40→  text: string;
    41→  status: string;
    42→  context?: string;
    43→  priority?: string;
    44→};
    45→
    46→type ObservationRow = {
    47→  id: RecordId<"observation", string>;
    48→  text: string;
    49→  severity: string;
    50→  status: string;
    51→  category?: string;
    52→};
    53→
    54→type AgentSessionRow = {
    55→  id: RecordId<"agent_session", string>;
    56→  agent: string;
    57→  ended_at?: Date | string;
    58→  summary?: string;
    59→};
    60→
    61→type SubtaskRow = {
    62→  id: RecordId<"task", string>;
    63→  title: string;
    64→  status: string;
    65→};
    66→
    67→type FeatureRow = {
    68→  id: RecordId<"feature", string>;
    69→  name: string;
    70→  description?: string;
    71→};
    72→
    73→type DependencyRow = {
    74→  id: RecordId<"task", string>;
    75→  title: string;
    76→  status: string;
    77→};
    78→
    79→function toId(record: RecordId<string, string>): string {
    80→  return `${record.table.name}:${record.id as string}`;
    81→}
    82→
    83→function toIso(value: Date | string | undefined): string {
    84→  if (!value) return "";
    85→  return value instanceof Date ? value.toISOString() : new Date(value).toISOString();
    86→}
    87→
    88→/** Build a broad project context packet (all decisions, tasks, questions for a project) */
    89→export async function buildProjectContext(input: {
    90→  surreal: Surreal;
    91→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    92→  workspaceName: string;
    93→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>;
    94→  taskId?: string;
    95→  since?: string;
    96→}): Promise<ContextPacket> {
    97→  const { surreal, workspaceRecord, workspaceName, projectRecord, taskId } = input;
    98→
    99→  // Load project details
   100→  const project = await surreal.select<ProjectRow>(projectRecord);
   101→  if (!project) {
   102→    throw new Error(`project not found: ${projectRecord.id}`);
   103→  }
   104→
   105→  // Load project-scoped entities in parallel
   106→  const projectEntitiesQuery = `
   107→    LET $project_entity_ids = SELECT VALUE \`in\` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project;
   108→
   109→    SELECT id, summary, status, rationale, decided_at, category, priority
   110→    FROM decision
   111→    WHERE workspace = $workspace AND id IN $project_entity_ids
   112→    ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 50;
   113→
   114→    SELECT id, title, status, priority, category, description
   115→    FROM task
   116→    WHERE workspace = $workspace AND id IN $project_entity_ids
   117→    ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 50;
   118→
   119→    SELECT id, text, status, context, priority
   120→    FROM question
   121→    WHERE workspace = $workspace AND id IN $project_entity_ids
   122→    ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 30;
   123→
   124→    SELECT id, text, severity, status, category
   125→    FROM observation
   126→    WHERE workspace = $workspace AND status IN ["open", "acknowledged"]
   127→    ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 20;
   128→  `;
   129→
   130→  const results = await surreal
   131→    .query<[null, DecisionRow[], TaskRow[], QuestionRow[], ObservationRow[]]>(projectEntitiesQuery, {
   132→      project: projectRecord,
   133→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   134→    })
   135→    .collect<[null, DecisionRow[], TaskRow[], QuestionRow[], ObservationRow[]]>();
   136→
   137→  const [, decisionRows, taskRows, questionRows, observationRows] = results;
   138→
   139→  // Categorize decisions by status
   140→  const confirmed: DecisionContext[] = [];
   141→  const provisional: DecisionContext[] = [];
   142→  const contested: DecisionContext[] = [];
   143→
   144→  for (const d of decisionRows) {
   145→    const ctx: DecisionContext = {
   146→      id: toId(d.id),
   147→      summary: d.summary,
   148→      status: d.status,
   149→      ...(d.rationale ? { rationale: d.rationale } : {}),
   150→      ...(d.decided_at ? { decided_at: toIso(d.decided_at) } : {}),
   151→      ...(d.category ? { category: d.category } : {}),
   152→      ...(d.priority ? { priority: d.priority } : {}),
   153→    };
   154→
   155→    if (d.status === "contested") {
   156→      contested.push(ctx);
   157→    } else if (d.status === "provisional" || d.status === "inferred") {
   158→      provisional.push(ctx);
   159→    } else if (d.status === "confirmed") {
   160→      confirmed.push(ctx);
   161→    }
   162→  }
   163→
   164→  // Map tasks
   165→  const activeTasks: TaskContext[] = taskRows
   166→    .filter((t) => t.status !== "done" && t.status !== "completed")
   167→    .map((t) => ({
   168→      id: toId(t.id),
   169→      title: t.title,
   170→      status: t.status,
   171→      ...(t.priority ? { priority: t.priority } : {}),
   172→      ...(t.category ? { category: t.category } : {}),
   173→    }));
   174→
   175→  // Map questions
   176→  const openQuestions: QuestionContext[] = questionRows
   177→    .filter((q) => q.status !== "answered" && q.status !== "resolved")
   178→    .map((q) => ({
   179→      id: toId(q.id),
   180→      text: q.text,
   181→      status: q.status,
   182→      ...(q.context ? { context: q.context } : {}),
   183→      ...(q.priority ? { priority: q.priority } : {}),
   184→    }));
   185→
   186→  // Map observations
   187→  const observations: ObservationContext[] = observationRows.map((o) => ({
   188→    id: toId(o.id),
   189→    text: o.text,
   190→    severity: o.severity,
   191→    status: o.status,
   192→    ...(o.category ? { category: o.category } : {}),
   193→  }));
   194→
   195→  // Build recent changes if `since` provided
   196→  const recentChanges: RecentChangeContext[] = input.since
   197→    ? await loadRecentChanges(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.since)
   198→    : [];
   199→
   200→  // Build task scope if taskId provided
   201→  let taskScope: TaskScopeContext | undefined;
   202→  if (taskId) {
   203→    taskScope = await buildTaskScope(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, taskId);
   204→  }
   205→
   206→  return {
   207→    workspace: { id: workspaceRecord.id as string, name: workspaceName },
   208→    project: {
   209→      id: projectRecord.id as string,
   210→      name: project.name,
   211→      status: project.status,
   212→      ...(project.description ? { description: project.description } : {}),
   213→    },
   214→    ...(taskScope ? { task_scope: taskScope } : {}),
   215→    decisions: { confirmed, provisional, contested },
   216→    active_tasks: activeTasks,
   217→    open_questions: openQuestions,
   218→    recent_changes: recentChanges,
   219→    observations,
   220→  };
   221→}
   222→
   223→/** Build task-scoped context: subtasks, parent feature, siblings, dependencies, related sessions */
   224→async function buildTaskScope(
   225→  surreal: Surreal,
   226→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   227→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>,
   228→  taskId: string,
   229→): Promise<TaskScopeContext> {
   230→  const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", taskId);
   231→  const task = await surreal.select<TaskRow>(taskRecord);
   232→  if (!task) {
   233→    throw new Error(`task not found: ${taskId}`);
   234→  }
   235→
   236→  // Load subtasks, parent feature, dependencies, and related sessions in parallel
   237→  const taskScopeQuery = `
   238→    -- Subtasks of this task
   239→    SELECT id, title, status
   240→    FROM task
   241→    WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE \`in\` FROM subtask_of WHERE out = $task)
   242→    ORDER BY created_at ASC;
   243→
   244→    -- Parent feature (via has_task edge)
   245→    SELECT id, name, description
   246→    FROM feature
   247→    WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE \`in\` FROM has_task WHERE out = $task)
   248→    LIMIT 1;
   249→
   250→    -- Dependencies (tasks this task depends on)
   251→    SELECT id, title, status
   252→    FROM task
   253→    WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM depends_on WHERE \`in\` = $task);
   254→
   255→    -- Recent agent sessions that touched this task
   256→    SELECT id, agent, ended_at, summary
   257→    FROM agent_session
   258→    WHERE task_id = $task AND ended_at != NONE
   259→    ORDER BY ended_at DESC LIMIT 5;
   260→  `;
   261→
   262→  const results = await surreal
   263→    .query<[SubtaskRow[], FeatureRow[], DependencyRow[], AgentSessionRow[]]>(taskScopeQuery, {
   264→      task: taskRecord,
   265→    })
   266→    .collect<[SubtaskRow[], FeatureRow[], DependencyRow[], AgentSessionRow[]]>();
   267→
   268→  const [subtaskRows, featureRows, dependencyRows, sessionRows] = results;
   269→
   270→  const parentFeature = featureRows.length > 0
   271→    ? {
   272→        id: toId(featureRows[0].id),
   273→        name: featureRows[0].name,
   274→        ...(featureRows[0].description ? { description: featureRows[0].description } : {}),
   275→      }
   276→    : undefined;
   277→
   278→  // Load sibling tasks (other tasks under same feature)
   279→  let siblingTasks: { id: string; title: string; status: string }[] = [];
   280→  if (parentFeature && featureRows.length > 0) {
   281→    const [siblingRows] = await surreal
   282→      .query<[TaskRow[]]>(
   283→        `SELECT id, title, status FROM task
   284→         WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_task WHERE \`in\` = $feature)
   285→           AND id != $task
   286→         ORDER BY created_at ASC LIMIT 20;`,
   287→        { feature: featureRows[0].id, task: taskRecord },
   288→      )
   289→      .collect<[TaskRow[]]>();
   290→
   291→    siblingTasks = siblingRows.map((t) => ({
   292→      id: toId(t.id),
   293→      title: t.title,
   294→      status: t.status,
   295→    }));
   296→  }
   297→
   298→  return {
   299→    task: {
   300→      id: toId(task.id),
   301→      title: task.title,
   302→      ...(task.description ? { description: task.description } : {}),
   303→      status: task.status,
   304→      ...(task.category ? { category: task.category } : {}),
   305→    },
   306→    subtasks: subtaskRows.map((s) => ({
   307→      id: toId(s.id),
   308→      title: s.title,
   309→      status: s.status,
   310→    })),
   311→    parent_feature: parentFeature,
   312→    sibling_tasks: siblingTasks,
   313→    dependencies: dependencyRows.map((d) => ({
   314→      id: toId(d.id),
   315→      title: d.title,
   316→      status: d.status,
   317→    })),
   318→    related_sessions: sessionRows.map((s) => ({
   319→      id: toId(s.id),
   320→      agent: s.agent,
   321→      ended_at: toIso(s.ended_at),
   322→      summary: s.summary ?? "",
   323→    })),
   324→  };
   325→}
   326→
   327→/** Load entities changed since a timestamp for the recent_changes field */
   328→async function loadRecentChanges(
   329→  surreal: Surreal,
   330→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   331→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>,
   332→  since: string,
   333→): Promise<RecentChangeContext[]> {
   334→  const sinceDate = new Date(since);
   335→
   336→  const query = `
   337→    LET $project_entity_ids = SELECT VALUE \`in\` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project;
   338→
   339→    LET $recent_decisions = SELECT "decision" AS entity_type, summary AS entity_name, status AS change_type, updated_at AS changed_at
   340→    FROM decision
   341→    WHERE workspace = $workspace AND id IN $project_entity_ids AND updated_at > $since
   342→    ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 20;
   343→
   344→    LET $recent_tasks = SELECT "task" AS entity_type, title AS entity_name, status AS change_type, updated_at AS changed_at
   345→    FROM task
   346→    WHERE workspace = $workspace AND id IN $project_entity_ids AND updated_at > $since
   347→    ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 20;
   348→
   349→    LET $recent_questions = SELECT "question" AS entity_type, text AS entity_name, status AS change_type, updated_at AS changed_at
   350→    FROM question
   351→    WHERE workspace = $workspace AND id IN $project_entity_ids AND updated_at > $since
   352→    ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 20;
   353→
   354→    RETURN array::flatten([$recent_decisions, $recent_tasks, $recent_questions]);
   355→  `;
   356→
   357→  const results = await surreal
   358→    .query<[null, null, null, null, RecentChangeRow[]]>(query, {
   359→      project: projectRecord,
   360→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   361→      since: sinceDate,
   362→    })
   363→    .collect<[null, null, null, null, RecentChangeRow[]]>();
   364→
   365→  const rows = results[4] ?? [];
   366→
   367→  return rows.map((r) => ({
   368→    entity_type: r.entity_type,
   369→    entity_name: r.entity_name,
   370→    change_type: r.change_type,
   371→    changed_at: toIso(r.changed_at),
   372→  }));
   373→}
   374→
   375→type RecentChangeRow = {
   376→  entity_type: string;
   377→  entity_name: string;
   378→  change_type: string;
   379→  changed_at: Date | string;
   380→};
   381→
```

     1→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     2→import { jsonError, jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
     3→import { logError, logInfo } from "../http/observability";
     4→import { buildProjectContext } from "./context-builder";
     5→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
     6→
     7→type WorkspaceRow = {
     8→  id: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
     9→  name: string;
    10→};
    11→
    12→type ProjectRow = {
    13→  id: RecordId<"project", string>;
    14→  name: string;
    15→};
    16→
    17→/**
    18→ * Creates MCP route handler.
    19→ *
    20→ * Endpoints:
    21→ *   POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/context
    22→ *     Body: { project_id: string, task_id?: string, since?: string }
    23→ *     Returns: ContextPacket
    24→ *
    25→ *   GET /api/mcp/:workspaceId/projects
    26→ *     Returns: { projects: { id, name }[] }
    27→ */
    28→export function createMcpRouteHandlers(deps: ServerDependencies) {
    29→  const { surreal } = deps;
    30→
    31→  async function handleGetContext(workspaceId: string, request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    32→    // Validate workspace
    33→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
    34→    const workspace = await surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
    35→    if (!workspace) {
    36→      return jsonError("workspace not found", 404);
    37→    }
    38→
    39→    // Parse body
    40→    let body: { project_id: string; task_id?: string; since?: string };
    41→    try {
    42→      body = await request.json();
    43→    } catch {
    44→      return jsonError("invalid JSON body", 400);
    45→    }
    46→
    47→    if (!body.project_id || typeof body.project_id !== "string") {
    48→      return jsonError("project_id is required", 400);
    49→    }
    50→
    51→    // Validate project belongs to workspace
    52→    const projectRecord = new RecordId("project", body.project_id);
    53→    const project = await surreal.select<ProjectRow>(projectRecord);
    54→    if (!project) {
    55→      return jsonError("project not found", 404);
    56→    }
    57→
    58→    try {
    59→      const contextPacket = await buildProjectContext({
    60→        surreal,
    61→        workspaceRecord,
    62→        workspaceName: workspace.name,
    63→        projectRecord,
    64→        taskId: body.task_id,
    65→        since: body.since,
    66→      });
    67→
    68→      logInfo("mcp.context.built", "MCP context packet assembled", {
    69→        workspaceId,
    70→        projectId: body.project_id,
    71→        taskId: body.task_id,
    72→        decisionsCount:
    73→          contextPacket.decisions.confirmed.length +
    74→          contextPacket.decisions.provisional.length +
    75→          contextPacket.decisions.contested.length,
    76→        tasksCount: contextPacket.active_tasks.length,
    77→        questionsCount: contextPacket.open_questions.length,
    78→        observationsCount: contextPacket.observations.length,
    79→      });
    80→
    81→      return jsonResponse(contextPacket, 200);
    82→    } catch (error) {
    83→      logError("mcp.context.failed", "Failed to build MCP context", error);
    84→      return jsonError("failed to build context", 500);
    85→    }
    86→  }
    87→
    88→  async function handleListProjects(workspaceId: string): Promise<Response> {
    89→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
    90→    const workspace = await surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
    91→    if (!workspace) {
    92→      return jsonError("workspace not found", 404);
    93→    }
    94→
    95→    const [projectRows] = await surreal
    96→      .query<[ProjectRow[]]>(
    97→        "SELECT id, name FROM project WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_project WHERE `in` = $workspace);",
    98→        { workspace: workspaceRecord },
    99→      )
   100→      .collect<[ProjectRow[]]>();
   101→
   102→    const projects = projectRows.map((p) => ({
   103→      id: p.id.id as string,
   104→      name: p.name,
   105→    }));
   106→
   107→    return jsonResponse({ workspace: { id: workspaceId, name: workspace.name }, projects }, 200);
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  return {
   111→    handleGetContext,
   112→    handleListProjects,
   113→  };
   114→}
   115→

Perfect! Now let me compile a comprehensive summary of all the patterns and code you need for implementing MCP API routes. Let me organize this into a clear reference document.

## Comprehensive MCP API Patterns Reference

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here are all the reusable patterns for implementing MCP HTTP API routes, CLI, and MCP stdio server:

### 1. **Request/Response Pattern** (`app/src/server/http/`)

**Response Helpers** (`response.ts`):
- `jsonError(message: string, status: number): Response` - for error responses
- `jsonResponse(payload: object, status: number): Response` - for success responses
- `withRequestIdHeader(response: Response, requestId: string): Response` - adds x-request-id header
- `toIsoString(value: Date | string): string` - ISO date formatting

**Request Logging Wrapper** (`request-logging.ts`):
- `withRequestLogging(route: string, method: string, handler: RouteHandler): RouteHandler`
- Automatically handles:
  - Request ID generation/retrieval from `x-request-id` header
  - Performance timing with `performance.now()`
  - Error boundary with fallback to 500 response
  - Request context setup via `runWithRequestContext()`
- Pattern: Wrap all route handlers with this middleware for unified logging

### 2. **Tool Execution Context** (`app/src/server/chat/tools/types.ts`)

```typescript
export type ChatToolExecutionContext = {
  actor: "chat_agent" | "mcp" | "pm_agent" | "analytics_agent";
  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
  currentMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
  latestUserText: string;
  workspaceOwnerRecord?: RecordId<"person", string>;
};

export type ChatToolDeps = {
  surreal: Surreal;
  embeddingModel: any;
  embeddingDimension: number;
  extractionModelId: string;
  extractionModel: any;
  extractionStoreThreshold: number;
};
```

Key insight: The context includes the **actor** field which can be set to `"mcp"` for MCP routes. This allows tool code to distinguish between different callers.

### 3. **Decision Creation Pattern** (`app/src/server/chat/tools/create-provisional-decision.ts`)

Essential steps for MCP decision write route:

```typescript
// 1. Resolve project/feature context
const projectRecord = input.project
  ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
      surreal: deps.surreal,
      workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
      projectInput: input.project,
    })
  : undefined;

// 2. Create decision record with key fields
const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({
  surreal: deps.surreal,
  summary: input.name,
  status: "provisional", // or "confirmed", "proposed", etc.
  now: new Date(),
  workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
  sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
  rationale: input.rationale,
  optionsConsidered: input.options_considered,
  decidedByName: "mcp", // mark origin
  projectRecord,
  featureRecord,
});

// 3. Create extraction provenance edge
await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
  surreal: deps.surreal,
  sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
  targetRecord: decisionRecord,
  now: new Date(),
  confidence: 0.9,
  model: deps.extractionModelId,
  fromText: input.name,
  evidence: context.latestUserText,
  evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
});

// 4. Return structured response
return {
  decision_id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
  status: "provisional",
  review_required: true,
};
```

**Key graph functions used:**
- `createDecisionRecord()` - creates decision with optional project/feature linking
- `createExtractionProvenanceEdge()` - tracks provenance with confidence scores
- `resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord()` - resolves project by ID or fuzzy name match
- `resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord()` - resolves feature by ID or fuzzy name match

### 4. **Constraint Checking Pattern** (`app/src/server/chat/tools/check-constraints.ts`)

For MCP constraints route:

```typescript
// 1. Create embedding for proposed action
const queryEmbedding = await createEmbeddingVector(
  deps.embeddingModel,
  input.proposed_action,
  deps.embeddingDimension,
);

// 2. Search for semantically similar constraints/decisions
const candidates = await listDecisionConstraintCandidates({
  surreal: deps.surreal,
  workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
  queryEmbedding,
  projectRecord, // optional project scope
  limit: 14,
});

// 3. Classify results into hard conflicts / soft tensions / supporting
const hardConflicts = [], softTensions = [], supporting = [];
for (const candidate of candidates) {
  const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);
  const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);
  
  if (candidate.kind === "decision" && overlap && 
      (status === "contested" || status === "superseded")) {
    hardConflicts.push({ ... });
  }
  
  if (candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {
    supporting.push({ ... });
  }
  
  if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {
    softTensions.push({ ... });
  }
}

return {
  hard_conflicts: hardConflicts,
  soft_tensions: softTensions,
  supporting,
  proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0,
};
```

**Key graph function:**
- `listDecisionConstraintCandidates()` - returns vector-ranked decisions/questions/tasks in workspace

### 5. **Embedding Generation** (`app/src/server/graph/embeddings.ts`)

```typescript
export async function createEmbeddingVector(
  embeddingModel: any,
  value: string,
  expectedDimension: number,
): Promise<number[] | undefined> {
  const normalized = value.trim();
  if (normalized.length === 0) return undefined;
  
  const result = await embed({
    model: embeddingModel,
    value: normalized,
  });
  
  if (result.embedding.length !== expectedDimension) {
    return undefined;
  }
  
  return result.embedding;
}

export function cosineSimilarity(a: number[], b: number[]): number {
  // Standard cosine similarity implementation
}
```

### 6. **Core Query Patterns** (`app/src/server/graph/queries.ts`)

**Entity Resolution (fuzzy + exact matching):**
```typescript
// parseRecordIdString(value, allowedTables, fallbackTable?)
// - parses "table:id" format or returns fallback record ID

// resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord, projectInput })
// - accepts "project:id" or fuzzy name ("Brain" matches "brain-planning")

// resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord, featureInput })
// - same pattern for features
```

**Search/Discovery:**
```typescript
// searchEntitiesByEmbedding(input)
// - returns RankedEntity[] with cosine similarity scores

// listDecisionConstraintCandidates(input)
// - specialized search for decisions/questions/tasks

// getEntityDetail(input)
// - returns full EntityDetail with relationships and provenance

// getProjectStatus(input)
// - aggregated project status (tasks, decisions, questions, features)

// getWorkspaceOwnerRecord(input)
// - resolve workspace owner person record
```

**Record Creation:**
```typescript
// createDecisionRecord(input)
// createQuestionRecord(input)
// createProjectRecord(input)
// - all return RecordId<table, string> and optionally create edges

// createExtractionProvenanceEdge(input)
// - creates extraction_relation edge with confidence, model, evidence
```

**Graph Traversal:**
```typescript
// listEntityNeighbors(input)
// - returns EntityNeighbor[] (incoming/outgoing, relation kind, confidence)

// getProjectGraphView(input)
// - collects all features/tasks/decisions for project

// getFocusedGraphView(input)
// - breadth-first traversal from center entity to depth N

// getWorkspaceGraphOverview(input)
// - projects + features for workspace-level visualization
```

### 7. **SurrealDB Schema Overview** (relevant tables)

**Core Entity Tables** (all SCHEMAFULL):
- `decision` - summary, rationale, status (extracted/proposed/confirmed/provisional/contested/superseded), decided_by_name, embedding
- `task` - title, description, status, priority (low/medium/high/critical), owner, deadline, workspace, embedding
- `question` - text, status, assigned_to, priority, workspace, embedding
- `project` - name, status, description, workspace, embedding
- `feature` - name, status, description, owner, category, embedding
- `person` - name, role, contact_email, identities, embedding
- `observation` - text, severity (info/warning/conflict), status (open/acknowledged/resolved), source_agent

**Relationship Tables** (TYPE RELATION):
- `belongs_to` - connects task/decision/question → project/feature
- `has_project` - workspace → project
- `has_feature` - project → feature
- `has_task` - feature → task
- `entity_relation` - generic workspace/project/person/task/decision/question → same (tracks semantic relationships)
- `extraction_relation` - message/document_chunk/git_commit → entities (provenance with confidence/model/evidence)
- `conflicts_with` - decision/feature → decision/feature
- `decided_in` - decision → conversation/meeting

**Key Indexes:**
- Vector indexes (HNSW DIMENSION 1536 DIST COSINE) on `embedding` fields for KNN search
- Workspace + status indexes for efficient filtering
- Unique indexes on external_id fields

### 8. **Server Dependencies** (`app/src/server/runtime/types.ts`)

```typescript
export type ServerDependencies = {
  config: ServerConfig;
  surreal: Surreal;
  analyticsSurreal: Surreal; // read-only for analytics agent
  chatAgentModel: any;
  extractionModel: any;
  pmAgentModel: any;
  analyticsAgentModel: any;
  embeddingModel: any;
  sse: SseRegistry;
};
```

The MCP routes will receive these dependencies injected.

### 9. **Package Dependencies** (`package.json`)

Key dependencies already in the project:
- `surrealdb`: "2.0.0-alpha.18" - SDK for SurrealDB
- `ai`: "^6.0.101" - Vercel AI SDK (for LLM calls, embeddings, tools)
- `zod`: "^4.3.6" - schema validation
- `pino`: "^10.3.1" - logging
- `@openrouter/ai-sdk-provider`: "^2.2.3" - OpenRouter LLM provider

### 10. **RecordId Usage Pattern**

Critical: Always use `RecordId` objects from the SDK, never string-based IDs:

```typescript
import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";

// Create new record
const decisionRecord = new RecordId("decision", randomUUID());
await surreal.create(decisionRecord).content({ ... });

// Select existing record
const row = await surreal.select<Type>(recordId);

// Create relation
await surreal.relate(fromRecord, new RecordId("relation_table", randomUUID()), toRecord, { ... })
  .output("after");

// Query with RecordId parameters
await surreal.query<[Result[]]>(
  "SELECT ... WHERE id = $record;",
  { record: someRecordId }
).collect<[Result[]]>();

// Access ID string when needed
const idString = recordId.id as string;

// Get table name (public API)
const tableName = recordId.table.name; // returns typed "task" | "decision" | etc.
```

### 11. **Implementation Checklist for MCP Routes**

For each MCP endpoint:

1. **Wrap with request logging**: Use `withRequestLogging(route, method, handler)`
2. **Validate inputs**: Use Zod schemas matching chat tool patterns
3. **Build execution context**: Create `ChatToolExecutionContext` with `actor: "mcp"`
4. **Resolve workspace**: Query SurrealDB to find workspace and validate workspace membership
5. **Create fake conversation/message records**: Decisions created via MCP need sourceMessageRecord (create placeholder message)
6. **Call graph functions**: Reuse `createDecisionRecord()`, `listDecisionConstraintCandidates()`, etc.
7. **Return typed responses**: Match tool response shapes (decision_id, status, etc.)
8. **Handle errors**: Throw early with descriptive messages caught by request-logging middleware

### 12. **Actor Context for MCP**

When creating tools or calling shared functions from MCP routes:

```typescript
const context: ChatToolExecutionContext = {
  actor: "mcp", // identifies as MCP caller
  workspaceRecord,
  conversationRecord: placeholderConversation, // create temporary conversation
  currentMessageRecord: placeholderMessage, // create temporary message
  latestUserText: input.summary || input.text, // use input as evidence
  workspaceOwnerRecord: ownerRecord,
};
```

This allows tool code to behave differently based on caller (chat_agent vs mcp vs pm_agent).

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import {
     4→  createDecisionRecord,
     5→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     6→  resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord,
     7→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     8→} from "../../graph/queries";
     9→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    10→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    11→
    12→export function createCreateProvisionalDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    13→  return tool({
    14→    description:
    15→      "Create a provisional decision when no existing answer exists. Use after resolve_decision returns unresolved and the user wants to proceed.",
    16→    inputSchema: z.object({
    17→      name: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise decision name"),
    18→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this decision was made"),
    19→      context: z
    20→        .object({
    21→          project: z.string().optional(),
    22→          feature: z.string().optional(),
    23→        })
    24→        .optional(),
    25→      options_considered: z.array(z.string().min(1)).optional(),
    26→    }),
    27→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    28→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    29→      const now = new Date();
    30→
    31→      const projectRecord = input.context?.project
    32→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    33→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    34→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    35→            projectInput: input.context.project,
    36→          })
    37→        : undefined;
    38→
    39→      const featureRecord = input.context?.feature
    40→        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({
    41→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    42→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    43→            featureInput: input.context.feature,
    44→          })
    45→        : undefined;
    46→
    47→      const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({
    48→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    49→        summary: input.name,
    50→        status: "provisional",
    51→        now,
    52→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    53→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    54→        rationale: input.rationale,
    55→        ...(input.options_considered && input.options_considered.length > 0
    56→          ? { optionsConsidered: input.options_considered }
    57→          : {}),
    58→        decidedByName: "chat_agent",
    59→        ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    60→        ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),
    61→      });
    62→
    63→      await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    64→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    65→        sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    66→        targetRecord: decisionRecord,
    67→        now,
    68→        confidence: 0.9,
    69→        model: deps.extractionModelId,
    70→        fromText: input.name,
    71→        evidence: context.latestUserText,
    72→        evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    73→      });
    74→
    75→      return {
    76→        decision_id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
    77→        status: "provisional",
    78→        review_required: true,
    79→      };
    80→    },
    81→  });
    82→}
    83→
```

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     4→import { listDecisionConstraintCandidates, resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord } from "../../graph/queries";
     5→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     6→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     7→
     8→function normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {
     9→  return new Set(
    10→    value
    11→      .toLowerCase()
    12→      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
    13→      .split(/\s+/)
    14→      .map((token) => token.trim())
    15→      .filter((token) => token.length > 2),
    16→  );
    17→}
    18→
    19→function hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {
    20→  for (const value of a) {
    21→    if (b.has(value)) {
    22→      return true;
    23→    }
    24→  }
    25→
    26→  return false;
    27→}
    28→
    29→export function createCheckConstraintsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    30→  return tool({
    31→    description:
    32→      "Check if a proposed action conflicts with existing decisions or constraints. Returns hard conflicts, soft tensions, supporting context, and proceed flag.",
    33→    inputSchema: z.object({
    34→      proposed_action: z.string().min(1).describe("What is being proposed"),
    35→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    36→    }),
    37→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    38→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    39→      const queryEmbedding = await createEmbeddingVector(
    40→        deps.embeddingModel,
    41→        input.proposed_action,
    42→        deps.embeddingDimension,
    43→      );
    44→      if (!queryEmbedding) {
    45→        throw new Error("failed to create query embedding for check_constraints");
    46→      }
    47→
    48→      const projectRecord = input.project
    49→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    50→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    51→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    52→            projectInput: input.project,
    53→          })
    54→        : undefined;
    55→
    56→      const candidates = await listDecisionConstraintCandidates({
    57→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    58→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    59→        queryEmbedding,
    60→        ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    61→        limit: 14,
    62→      });
    63→
    64→      const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(input.proposed_action);
    65→      const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    66→      const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    67→      const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    68→
    69→      for (const candidate of candidates) {
    70→        const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);
    71→        const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);
    72→        const status = candidate.status?.toLowerCase();
    73→
    74→        if (candidate.kind === "decision" && overlap && (status === "contested" || status === "superseded")) {
    75→          hardConflicts.push({
    76→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    77→            name: candidate.name,
    78→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    79→            reason: `Decision is marked ${status}.`,
    80→          });
    81→          continue;
    82→        }
    83→
    84→        if (candidate.kind === "decision" && candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {
    85→          supporting.push({
    86→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    87→            name: candidate.name,
    88→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    89→            reason: "High-similarity decision aligns with proposed action.",
    90→          });
    91→          continue;
    92→        }
    93→
    94→        if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {
    95→          softTensions.push({
    96→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    97→            name: candidate.name,
    98→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    99→            reason: overlap
   100→              ? "Related decision/question may require consistency checks."
   101→              : "Semantically related context may be affected.",
   102→          });
   103→        }
   104→      }
   105→
   106→      return {
   107→        hard_conflicts: hardConflicts,
   108→        soft_tensions: softTensions,
   109→        supporting,
   110→        proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0,
   111→      };
   112→    },
   113→  });
   114→}
   115→
```

     1→export function jsonError(message: string, status: number): Response {
     2→  return jsonResponse({ error: message }, status);
     3→}
     4→
     5→export function jsonResponse(payload: object, status: number): Response {
     6→  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload), {
     7→    status,
     8→    headers: {
     9→      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    10→      "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
    11→    },
    12→  });
    13→}
    14→
    15→export function withRequestIdHeader(response: Response, requestId: string): Response {
    16→  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
    17→  headers.set("x-request-id", requestId);
    18→
    19→  return new Response(response.body, {
    20→    status: response.status,
    21→    statusText: response.statusText,
    22→    headers,
    23→  });
    24→}
    25→
    26→export function toIsoString(value: Date | string): string {
    27→  if (value instanceof Date) {
    28→    return value.toISOString();
    29→  }
    30→
    31→  const parsed = new Date(value);
    32→  if (!Number.isNaN(parsed.valueOf())) {
    33→    return parsed.toISOString();
    34→  }
    35→
    36→  return value;
    37→}
    38→

   104→
   105→export type GraphViewRawEntity = {
   106→  id: string;
   107→  kind: GraphEntityTable;
   108→  name: string;
   109→};
   110→
   111→export type GraphViewRawEdge = {
   112→  id: string;
   113→  kind: string;
   114→  fromId: string;
   115→  toId: string;
   116→  confidence: number;
   117→};
   118→
   119→export type GraphViewRawResult = {
   120→  entities: GraphViewRawEntity[];
   121→  edges: GraphViewRawEdge[];
   122→};
   123→
   124→type EntitySearchCandidate = {
   125→  id: GraphEntityRecord;
   126→  kind: SearchEntityKind;
   127→  name: string;
   128→  status?: string;
   129→  embedding?: number[];
   130→};
   131→
   132→const SEARCH_KINDS: SearchEntityKind[] = ["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"];
   133→
   134→function toRecordIdString(record: RecordId<string, string>): string {
   135→  return record.id as string;
   136→}
   137→
   138→function toIsoString(value: string | Date): string {
   139→  return value instanceof Date ? value.toISOString() : new Date(value).toISOString();
   140→}
   141→
   142→function normalizeSearchValue(value: string): string {
   143→  return value
   144→    .trim()
   145→    .toLowerCase()
   146→    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
   147→    .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   148→}
   149→
   150→export function parseRecordIdString<T extends string>(
   151→  value: string,
   152→  allowedTables: T[],
   153→  fallbackTable?: T,
   154→): RecordId<T, string> {
   155→  const normalized = value.trim();
   156→  if (normalized.length === 0) {
   157→    throw new Error("record id is required");
   158→  }
   159→
   160→  const separatorIndex = normalized.indexOf(":");
   161→  if (separatorIndex === -1) {
   162→    if (!fallbackTable) {
   163→      throw new Error(`record id must be prefixed with one of: ${allowedTables.join(", ")}`);
   164→    }
   165→
   166→    return new RecordId(fallbackTable, normalized) as RecordId<T, string>;
   167→  }
   168→
   169→  const table = normalized.slice(0, separatorIndex) as T;
   170→  const id = normalized.slice(separatorIndex + 1);
   171→  if (!allowedTables.includes(table)) {
   172→    throw new Error(`record id table must be one of: ${allowedTables.join(", ")}`);
   173→  }
   174→
   175→  if (id.length === 0) {
   176→    throw new Error("record id is missing its identifier component");
   177→  }
   178→
   179→  return new RecordId(table, id) as RecordId<T, string>;
   180→}
   181→
   182→export async function readEntityName(
   183→  surreal: Surreal,
   184→  record: GraphEntityRecord,
   185→): Promise<string | undefined> {
   186→  const table = record.table.name;
   187→
   188→  if (table === "workspace" || table === "project" || table === "person" || table === "feature") {
   189→    const row = await surreal.select<{ name: string }>(record as RecordId<typeof table, string>);
   190→    return row?.name;
   191→  }
   192→
   193→  if (table === "task") {
   194→    const row = await surreal.select<{ title: string }>(record as RecordId<"task", string>);
   195→    return row?.title;
   196→  }
   197→
   198→  if (table === "decision") {
   199→    const row = await surreal.select<{ summary: string }>(record as RecordId<"decision", string>);
   200→    return row?.summary;
   201→  }
   202→
   203→  const row = await surreal.select<{ text: string }>(record as RecordId<"question", string>);
   204→  return row?.text;
   205→}
   206→
   207→async function getWorkspaceProjectRows(
   208→  surreal: Surreal,
   209→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   210→): Promise<Array<{ id: RecordId<"project", string>; name: string }>> {
   211→  const [rows] = await surreal
   212→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"project", string>; name: string }>]>(
   213→      "SELECT id, name FROM project WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_project WHERE `in` = $workspace);",
   214→      { workspace: workspaceRecord },
   215→    )
   216→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"project", string>; name: string }>]>();
   217→
   218→  return rows;
   219→}
   220→
   221→async function getWorkspaceMessageIds(
   222→  surreal: Surreal,
   223→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   224→): Promise<RecordId<"message", string>[]> {
   225→  const [rows] = await surreal
   226→    .query<[Array<RecordId<"message", string>>]>(
   227→      "SELECT VALUE id FROM message WHERE conversation IN (SELECT VALUE id FROM conversation WHERE workspace = $workspace);",
   228→      { workspace: workspaceRecord },
   229→    )
   230→    .collect<[Array<RecordId<"message", string>>]>();
   231→
   232→  return rows;
   233→}
   234→
   235→export async function isEntityInWorkspace(
   236→  surreal: Surreal,
   237→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   238→  entityRecord: GraphEntityRecord,
   239→): Promise<boolean> {
   240→  const table = entityRecord.table.name;
   241→
   242→  if (table === "workspace") {
   243→    return toRecordIdString(entityRecord) === toRecordIdString(workspaceRecord);
   244→  }
   245→
   246→  if (table === "project") {
   247→    const [rows] = await surreal
   248→      .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"has_project", string> }>]>(
   249→        "SELECT id FROM has_project WHERE `in` = $workspace AND out = $project LIMIT 1;",
   250→        { workspace: workspaceRecord, project: entityRecord },
   251→      )
   252→      .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"has_project", string> }>]>();
   253→
   254→    return rows.length > 0;
   255→  }
   256→
   257→  if (table === "person") {
   258→    const [rows] = await surreal
   259→      .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"member_of", string> }>]>(
   260→        "SELECT id FROM member_of WHERE `in` = $person AND out = $workspace LIMIT 1;",
   261→        { workspace: workspaceRecord, person: entityRecord },
   262→      )
   263→      .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"member_of", string> }>]>();
   264→
   265→    return rows.length > 0;
   266→  }
   267→
   268→  if (table === "feature") {
   269→    const [rows] = await surreal
   270→      .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string> }>]>(
   271→        [
   272→          "SELECT id",
   273→          "FROM feature",
   274→          "WHERE id = $feature",
   275→          "AND id IN (",
   276→          "  SELECT VALUE out",
   277→          "  FROM has_feature",
   278→          "  WHERE `in` IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_project WHERE `in` = $workspace)",
   279→          ");",
   280→        ].join(" "),
   281→        { workspace: workspaceRecord, feature: entityRecord },
   282→      )
   283→      .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string> }>]>();
   284→
   285→    return rows.length > 0;
   286→  }
   287→
   288→  if (table === "task" || table === "decision" || table === "question") {
   289→    const [rows] = await surreal
   290→      .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task" | "decision" | "question", string> }>]>(
   291→        `SELECT id FROM ${table} WHERE id = $entity AND workspace = $workspace;`,
   292→        { workspace: workspaceRecord, entity: entityRecord },
   293→      )
   294→      .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task" | "decision" | "question", string> }>]>();
   295→
   296→    return rows.length > 0;
   297→  }
   298→
   299→  return false;

   357→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::entity_search_workspace($q: string, $limit: int, $workspace: record<workspace>) {
   358→  LET $query = string::lowercase(string::trim($q));
   359→  LET $safe_limit = math::min([math::max([$limit, 1]), 100]);
   360→
   361→  LET $task_rows = SELECT
   362→    id,
   363→    "task" AS kind,
   364→    title AS text,
   365→    extraction_confidence AS confidence,
   366→    source_message AS sourceMessage,
   367→    priority,
   368→    created_at
   369→  FROM task
   370→  WHERE workspace = $workspace
   371→    AND extraction_confidence != NONE
   372→    AND string::contains(string::lowercase(title), $query)
   373→  ORDER BY created_at DESC
   374→  LIMIT $safe_limit;
   375→
   376→  LET $decision_rows = SELECT
   377→    id,
   378→    "decision" AS kind,
   379→    summary AS text,
   380→    extraction_confidence AS confidence,
   381→    source_message AS sourceMessage,
   382→    priority,
   383→    created_at
   384→  FROM decision
   385→  WHERE workspace = $workspace
   386→    AND extraction_confidence != NONE
   387→    AND string::contains(string::lowercase(summary), $query)
   388→  ORDER BY created_at DESC
   389→  LIMIT $safe_limit;
   390→
   391→  LET $question_rows = SELECT
   392→    id,
   393→    "question" AS kind,
   394→    text AS text,
   395→    extraction_confidence AS confidence,
   396→    source_message AS sourceMessage,
   397→    priority,
   398→    created_at
   399→  FROM question
   400→  WHERE workspace = $workspace
   401→    AND extraction_confidence != NONE
   402→    AND string::contains(string::lowercase(text), $query)
   403→  ORDER BY created_at DESC
   404→  LIMIT $safe_limit;
   405→
   406→  RETURN array::flatten([$task_rows, $decision_rows, $question_rows]);
   407→};
   408→
   409→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::entity_search_project($q: string, $limit: int, $workspace: record<workspace>, $project: record<project>) {
   410→  LET $query = string::lowercase(string::trim($q));
   411→  LET $safe_limit = math::min([math::max([$limit, 1]), 100]);
   412→  LET $project_entity_ids = SELECT VALUE `in` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project;
   413→
   414→  LET $task_rows = SELECT
   415→    id,
   416→    "task" AS kind,
   417→    title AS text,
   418→    extraction_confidence AS confidence,
   419→    source_message AS sourceMessage,
   420→    priority,
   421→    created_at
   422→  FROM task
   423→  WHERE workspace = $workspace
   424→    AND extraction_confidence != NONE
   425→    AND id IN $project_entity_ids
   426→    AND string::contains(string::lowercase(title), $query)
   427→  ORDER BY created_at DESC
   428→  LIMIT $safe_limit;
   429→
   430→  LET $decision_rows = SELECT
   431→    id,
   432→    "decision" AS kind,
   433→    summary AS text,
   434→    extraction_confidence AS confidence,
   435→    source_message AS sourceMessage,
   436→    priority,
   437→    created_at
   438→  FROM decision
   439→  WHERE workspace = $workspace
   440→    AND extraction_confidence != NONE
   441→    AND id IN $project_entity_ids
   442→    AND string::contains(string::lowercase(summary), $query)
   443→  ORDER BY created_at DESC
   444→  LIMIT $safe_limit;
   445→
   446→  LET $question_rows = SELECT
   447→    id,
   448→    "question" AS kind,
   449→    text AS text,
   450→    extraction_confidence AS confidence,
   451→    source_message AS sourceMessage,
   452→    priority,
   453→    created_at
   454→  FROM question
   455→  WHERE workspace = $workspace
   456→    AND extraction_confidence != NONE
   457→    AND id IN $project_entity_ids
   458→    AND string::contains(string::lowercase(text), $query)
   459→  ORDER BY created_at DESC
   460→  LIMIT $safe_limit;
   461→
   462→  RETURN array::flatten([$task_rows, $decision_rows, $question_rows]);
   463→};
   464→
   465→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::conversation_recent($conversation: record<conversation>, $limit: int) {
   466→  LET $safe_limit = math::min([math::max([$limit, 1]), 50]);
   467→  RETURN SELECT id, role, text, createdAt
   468→  FROM message
   469→  WHERE conversation = $conversation
   470→  ORDER BY createdAt DESC
   471→  LIMIT $safe_limit;
   472→};
   473→
   474→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::entity_neighbors($entity: record) {
   475→  RETURN SELECT
   476→    ->entity_relation->(workspace, project, person, feature, task, decision, question) AS outgoing,
   477→    <-entity_relation<-(workspace, project, person, feature, task, decision, question) AS incoming
   478→  FROM $entity;
   479→};

  1264→export async function createDecisionRecord(input: {
  1265→  surreal: Surreal;
  1266→  summary: string;
  1267→  status: string;
  1268→  now: Date;
  1269→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
  1270→  sourceMessageRecord?: RecordId<"message", string>;
  1271→  rationale?: string;
  1272→  optionsConsidered?: string[];
  1273→  basedOn?: GraphEntityRecord[];
  1274→  inferredBy?: string;
  1275→  decidedByName?: string;
  1276→  projectRecord?: RecordId<"project", string>;
  1277→  featureRecord?: RecordId<"feature", string>;
  1278→}): Promise<RecordId<"decision", string>> {
  1279→  const decisionRecord = new RecordId("decision", randomUUID());
  1280→
  1281→  await input.surreal.create(decisionRecord).content({
  1282→    summary: input.summary,
  1283→    status: input.status,
  1284→    created_at: input.now,
  1285→    updated_at: input.now,
  1286→    workspace: input.workspaceRecord,
  1287→    ...(input.sourceMessageRecord ? { source_message: input.sourceMessageRecord } : {}),
  1288→    ...(input.rationale ? { rationale: input.rationale } : {}),
  1289→    ...(input.optionsConsidered && input.optionsConsidered.length > 0
  1290→      ? { options_considered: input.optionsConsidered }
  1291→      : {}),
  1292→    ...(input.basedOn && input.basedOn.length > 0 ? { based_on: input.basedOn } : {}),
  1293→    ...(input.inferredBy ? { inferred_by: input.inferredBy } : {}),
  1294→    ...(input.decidedByName ? { decided_by_name: input.decidedByName } : {}),
  1295→  });
  1296→
  1297→  if (input.projectRecord) {
  1298→    await input.surreal.relate(decisionRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), input.projectRecord, {
  1299→      added_at: input.now,
  1300→    }).output("after");
  1301→  }
  1302→
  1303→  if (input.featureRecord) {
  1304→    await input.surreal.relate(decisionRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), input.featureRecord, {
  1305→      added_at: input.now,
  1306→    }).output("after");
  1307→  }
  1308→
  1309→  return decisionRecord;
  1310→}

  1380→export async function createExtractionProvenanceEdge(input: {
  1381→  surreal: Surreal;
  1382→  sourceRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
  1383→  targetRecord: GraphEntityRecord;
  1384→  now: Date;
  1385→  confidence: number;
  1386→  model: string;
  1387→  fromText: string;
  1388→  evidence: string;
  1389→  evidenceSourceRecord?: RecordId<"message", string>;
  1390→  resolvedFromRecord?: RecordId<"message", string>;
  1391→}): Promise<RecordId<"extraction_relation", string>> {

  1393→
  1394→  await input.surreal
  1395→    .relate(input.sourceRecord, edgeRecord, input.targetRecord, {
  1396→      confidence: input.confidence,
  1397→      extracted_at: input.now,
  1398→      created_at: input.now,
  1399→      model: input.model,
  1400→      from_text: input.fromText,
  1401→      evidence: input.evidence,
  1402→      ...(input.evidenceSourceRecord ? { evidence_source: input.evidenceSourceRecord } : {}),
  1403→      ...(input.resolvedFromRecord ? { resolved_from: input.resolvedFromRecord } : {}),
  1404→    })
  1405→    .output("after");
  1406→
  1407→  return edgeRecord;
  1408→}

  1410→export async function getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage(input: {
  1411→  surreal: Surreal;
  1412→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
  1413→  decisionRecord: RecordId<"decision", string>;
  1414→}): Promise<RecordId<"message", string> | undefined> {
  1415→  const decision = await input.surreal.select<{ source_message?: RecordId<"message", string> }>(input.decisionRecord);
  1416→  if (decision?.source_message) {
  1417→    const workspaceMessageIds = new Set((await getWorkspaceMessageIds(input.surreal, input.workspaceRecord)).map((row) => toRecordIdString(row)));
  1418→    if (workspaceMessageIds.has(toRecordIdString(decision.source_message))) {
  1419→      return decision.source_message;
  1420→    }
  1421→  }
  1422→
  1423→  const [rows] = await input.surreal
  1424→    .query<[Array<{ in: RecordId<"message", string> }>]>(
  1425→      [
  1426→        "SELECT `in`",
  1427→        "FROM extraction_relation",
  1428→        "WHERE out = $decision",
  1429→        "AND `in` IN (",
  1430→        "  SELECT VALUE id",
  1431→        "  FROM message",
  1432→        "  WHERE conversation IN (SELECT VALUE id FROM conversation WHERE workspace = $workspace)",
  1433→        ")",
  1434→        "ORDER BY extracted_at ASC",
  1435→        "LIMIT 1;",
  1436→      ].join(" "),
  1437→      {
  1438→        workspace: input.workspaceRecord,
  1439→        decision: input.decisionRecord,
  1440→      },
  1441→    )
  1442→    .collect<[Array<{ in: RecordId<"message", string> }>]>();
  1443→
  1444→  return rows[0]?.in;
  1445→}
  1446→
  1447→export async function getWorkspaceOwnerRecord(input: {
  1448→  surreal: Surreal;
  1449→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
  1450→}): Promise<RecordId<"person", string> | undefined> {
  1451→  const [rows] = await input.surreal
  1452→    .query<[Array<RecordId<"person", string>>]>(
  1453→      "SELECT VALUE `in` FROM member_of WHERE out = $workspace AND role = 'owner' LIMIT 1;",
  1454→      { workspace: input.workspaceRecord },
  1455→    )
  1456→    .collect<[Array<RecordId<"person", string>>]>();
  1457→
  1458→  return rows[0];
  1459→}

  1461→export async function getDecisionRecordForWorkspace(input: {
  1462→  surreal: Surreal;
  1463→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
  1464→  decisionInput: string;
  1465→}): Promise<{ id: RecordId<"decision", string>; summary: string; status: string }> {
  1466→  const decisionRecord = parseRecordIdString(input.decisionInput, ["decision"], "decision");
  1467→  const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(input.surreal, input.workspaceRecord, decisionRecord);
  1468→  if (!scoped) {
  1469→    throw new Error("decision is outside the current workspace scope");
  1470→  }
  1471→
  1472→  const row = await input.surreal.select<{ id: RecordId<"decision", string>; summary: string; status: string }>(decisionRecord);
  1473→  if (!row) {
  1474→    throw new Error(`decision not found: ${input.decisionInput}`);
  1475→  }
  1476→
  1477→  return row;
  1478→}

  1480→export async function confirmDecisionRecord(input: {
  1481→  surreal: Surreal;
  1482→  decisionRecord: RecordId<"decision", string>;
  1483→  confirmedAt: Date;
  1484→  confirmedBy?: RecordId<"person", string>;
  1485→  notes?: string;
  1486→}): Promise<void> {
  1487→  await input.surreal.update(input.decisionRecord).merge({
  1488→    status: "confirmed",
  1489→    confirmed_at: input.confirmedAt,
  1490→    ...(input.confirmedBy ? { confirmed_by: input.confirmedBy } : {}),
  1491→    ...(input.notes ? { confirmation_notes: input.notes } : {}),
  1492→    updated_at: input.confirmedAt,
  1493→  });
  1494→}
  1495→
  1496→export async function listDecisionConstraintCandidates(input: {
  1497→  surreal: Surreal;
  1498→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
  1499→  queryEmbedding: number[];
  1500→  projectRecord?: RecordId<"project", string>;
  1501→  limit: number;
  1502→}): Promise<RankedEntity[]> {
  1503→  return searchEntitiesByEmbedding({
  1504→    surreal: input.surreal,
  1505→    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
  1506→    queryEmbedding: input.queryEmbedding,
  1507→    kinds: ["decision", "question", "task"],
  1508→    ...(input.projectRecord ? { projectRecord: input.projectRecord } : {}),

  1510→  });

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/minnetonka-v1/app/src/server/mcp/api-key.ts",
  "content": "import { randomBytes } from \"node:crypto\";\n\nconst API_KEY_PREFIX = \"brain_\";\nconst API_KEY_BYTE_LENGTH = 32;\n\n/** Generate a new API key with prefix. Returns the raw key (show once to user). */\nexport function generateApiKey(): string {\n  const bytes = randomBytes(API_KEY_BYTE_LENGTH);\n  return `${API_KEY_PREFIX}${bytes.toString(\"hex\")}`;\n}\n\n/** Hash an API key for storage. Uses Bun's built-in bcrypt. */\nexport async function hashApiKey(key: string): Promise<string> {\n  return Bun.password.hash(key, { algorithm: \"bcrypt\", cost: 10 });\n}\n\n/** Verify a raw API key against a stored hash. */\nexport async function verifyApiKey(key: string, hash: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  return Bun.password.verify(key, hash);\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/minnetonka-v1/app/src/server/mcp/auth.ts",
  "content": "import { RecordId, type Surreal } from \"surrealdb\";\nimport { verifyApiKey } from \"./api-key\";\nimport { jsonError } from \"../http/response\";\n\ntype WorkspaceRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  name: string;\n  api_key_hash?: string;\n};\n\nexport type McpAuthResult = {\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  workspaceName: string;\n};\n\n/**\n * Authenticate an MCP request via Bearer token.\n * Checks the Authorization header against workspace API key hashes.\n * Returns workspace context on success, or an error Response.\n */\nexport async function authenticateMcpRequest(\n  request: Request,\n  workspaceId: string,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n): Promise<McpAuthResult | Response> {\n  const authHeader = request.headers.get(\"authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return jsonError(\"missing or invalid Authorization header\", 401);\n  }\n\n  const apiKey = authHeader.slice(7);\n  if (!apiKey) {\n    return jsonError(\"empty API key\", 401);\n  }\n\n  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", workspaceId);\n  const workspace = await surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);\n  if (!workspace) {\n    return jsonError(\"workspace not found\", 404);\n  }\n\n  if (!workspace.api_key_hash) {\n    return jsonError(\"workspace has no API key configured — run brain init\", 403);\n  }\n\n  const valid = await verifyApiKey(apiKey, workspace.api_key_hash);\n  if (!valid) {\n    return jsonError(\"invalid API key\", 401);\n  }\n\n  return {\n    workspaceRecord,\n    workspaceName: workspace.name,\n  };\n}\n"
}
