{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/minnetonka-v1/app/src/server/mcp/mcp-queries.ts",
  "content": "import { randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { RecordId, type Surreal } from \"surrealdb\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Row types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntype DecisionRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"decision\", string>;\n  summary: string;\n  status: string;\n  rationale?: string;\n  decided_at?: Date | string;\n  category?: string;\n  priority?: string;\n  created_at: Date | string;\n};\n\ntype TaskRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"task\", string>;\n  title: string;\n  status: string;\n  priority?: string;\n  category?: string;\n  description?: string;\n  created_at: Date | string;\n};\n\ntype QuestionRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"question\", string>;\n  text: string;\n  status: string;\n  asked_by?: string;\n  options?: string[];\n  answer_summary?: string;\n  created_at: Date | string;\n};\n\ntype ConstraintRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"decision\" | \"observation\", string>;\n  text: string;\n  source: string;\n  severity: string;\n};\n\ntype EntityChangeRow = {\n  entity_type: string;\n  entity_name: string;\n  change_type: string;\n  changed_at: Date | string;\n};\n\ntype SubtaskRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"task\", string>;\n  title: string;\n  status: string;\n};\n\ntype DependencyRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"task\", string>;\n  title: string;\n  status: string;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction toId(record: RecordId<string, string>): string {\n  return `${record.table.name}:${record.id as string}`;\n}\n\nfunction toIso(value: Date | string | undefined): string {\n  if (!value) return \"\";\n  return value instanceof Date ? value.toISOString() : new Date(value).toISOString();\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Project-scoped queries\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** List active decisions for a project, optionally filtered by area/category */\nexport async function listProjectDecisions(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  projectRecord: RecordId<\"project\", string>;\n  area?: string;\n}): Promise<{\n  confirmed: Array<{ id: string; summary: string; status: string; rationale?: string; decided_at?: string; category?: string }>;\n  provisional: Array<{ id: string; summary: string; status: string; rationale?: string; decided_at?: string; category?: string }>;\n  contested: Array<{ id: string; summary: string; status: string; rationale?: string; decided_at?: string; category?: string }>;\n}> {\n  const areaFilter = input.area\n    ? `AND category = $area`\n    : \"\";\n\n  const [rows] = await input.surreal\n    .query<[DecisionRow[]]>(\n      `SELECT id, summary, status, rationale, decided_at, category, priority, created_at\n       FROM decision\n       WHERE workspace = $workspace\n         AND id IN (SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project)\n         ${areaFilter}\n       ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 50;`,\n      {\n        workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n        project: input.projectRecord,\n        ...(input.area ? { area: input.area } : {}),\n      },\n    )\n    .collect<[DecisionRow[]]>();\n\n  const confirmed: typeof result.confirmed = [];\n  const provisional: typeof result.provisional = [];\n  const contested: typeof result.contested = [];\n\n  for (const d of rows) {\n    const item = {\n      id: toId(d.id),\n      summary: d.summary,\n      status: d.status,\n      ...(d.rationale ? { rationale: d.rationale } : {}),\n      ...(d.decided_at ? { decided_at: toIso(d.decided_at) } : {}),\n      ...(d.category ? { category: d.category } : {}),\n    };\n\n    if (d.status === \"contested\") contested.push(item);\n    else if (d.status === \"provisional\" || d.status === \"inferred\") provisional.push(item);\n    else if (d.status === \"confirmed\") confirmed.push(item);\n  }\n\n  const result = { confirmed, provisional, contested };\n  return result;\n}\n\n/** Get task dependency tree (recursive) */\nexport async function getTaskDependencyTree(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  taskRecord: RecordId<\"task\", string>;\n}): Promise<{\n  task: { id: string; title: string; status: string };\n  dependencies: Array<{ id: string; title: string; status: string; resolved: boolean }>;\n  dependents: Array<{ id: string; title: string; status: string }>;\n  subtasks: Array<{ id: string; title: string; status: string }>;\n}> {\n  const task = await input.surreal.select<TaskRow>(input.taskRecord);\n  if (!task) throw new Error(`task not found: ${input.taskRecord.id}`);\n\n  const query = `\n    -- Tasks this task depends on\n    SELECT id, title, status\n    FROM task\n    WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM depends_on WHERE \\`in\\` = $task);\n\n    -- Tasks that depend on this task\n    SELECT id, title, status\n    FROM task\n    WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM depends_on WHERE out = $task);\n\n    -- Subtasks\n    SELECT id, title, status\n    FROM task\n    WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM subtask_of WHERE out = $task)\n    ORDER BY created_at ASC;\n  `;\n\n  const results = await input.surreal\n    .query<[DependencyRow[], DependencyRow[], SubtaskRow[]]>(query, { task: input.taskRecord })\n    .collect<[DependencyRow[], DependencyRow[], SubtaskRow[]]>();\n\n  const [deps, dependents, subtasks] = results;\n\n  return {\n    task: { id: toId(task.id), title: task.title, status: task.status },\n    dependencies: deps.map((d) => ({\n      id: toId(d.id),\n      title: d.title,\n      status: d.status,\n      resolved: d.status === \"done\" || d.status === \"completed\",\n    })),\n    dependents: dependents.map((d) => ({ id: toId(d.id), title: d.title, status: d.status })),\n    subtasks: subtasks.map((s) => ({ id: toId(s.id), title: s.title, status: s.status })),\n  };\n}\n\n/** List architecture constraints for a project */\nexport async function listProjectConstraints(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  projectRecord: RecordId<\"project\", string>;\n  area?: string;\n}): Promise<Array<{ text: string; source: string; severity: \"hard\" | \"soft\" }>> {\n  // Constraints come from two sources:\n  // 1. Decisions marked as confirmed/contested (hard constraints)\n  // 2. Observations with severity \"conflict\" or \"warning\"\n\n  const areaFilter = input.area ? `AND category = $area` : \"\";\n\n  const query = `\n    LET $project_entity_ids = SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project;\n\n    SELECT summary AS text, \"decision\" AS source_type, status, id\n    FROM decision\n    WHERE workspace = $workspace\n      AND id IN $project_entity_ids\n      AND status IN [\"confirmed\", \"contested\"]\n      ${areaFilter}\n    ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 30;\n\n    SELECT text, \"observation\" AS source_type, severity, id\n    FROM observation\n    WHERE workspace = $workspace\n      AND status IN [\"open\", \"acknowledged\"]\n      AND severity IN [\"conflict\", \"warning\"]\n    ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 20;\n  `;\n\n  const results = await input.surreal\n    .query<[null, Array<{ text: string; source_type: string; status: string; id: RecordId }>, Array<{ text: string; source_type: string; severity: string; id: RecordId }>]>(\n      query,\n      {\n        workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n        project: input.projectRecord,\n        ...(input.area ? { area: input.area } : {}),\n      },\n    )\n    .collect<[null, Array<{ text: string; source_type: string; status: string; id: RecordId }>, Array<{ text: string; source_type: string; severity: string; id: RecordId }>]>();\n\n  const [, decisionRows, observationRows] = results;\n\n  const constraints: Array<{ text: string; source: string; severity: \"hard\" | \"soft\" }> = [];\n\n  for (const d of decisionRows) {\n    constraints.push({\n      text: d.text,\n      source: `decision:${(d.id as RecordId<string, string>).id as string}`,\n      severity: d.status === \"contested\" ? \"hard\" : \"hard\",\n    });\n  }\n\n  for (const o of observationRows) {\n    constraints.push({\n      text: o.text,\n      source: `observation:${(o.id as RecordId<string, string>).id as string}`,\n      severity: o.severity === \"conflict\" ? \"hard\" : \"soft\",\n    });\n  }\n\n  return constraints;\n}\n\n/** List recent changes since a timestamp */\nexport async function listRecentChanges(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  projectRecord?: RecordId<\"project\", string>;\n  since: string;\n}): Promise<Array<{ entity_type: string; entity_name: string; change_type: string; changed_at: string }>> {\n  const sinceDate = new Date(input.since);\n\n  const projectFilter = input.projectRecord\n    ? `AND id IN (SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project)`\n    : \"\";\n\n  const query = `\n    LET $recent_decisions = SELECT \"decision\" AS entity_type, summary AS entity_name, status AS change_type, updated_at AS changed_at\n    FROM decision\n    WHERE workspace = $workspace ${projectFilter} AND updated_at > $since\n    ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 20;\n\n    LET $recent_tasks = SELECT \"task\" AS entity_type, title AS entity_name, status AS change_type, updated_at AS changed_at\n    FROM task\n    WHERE workspace = $workspace ${projectFilter} AND updated_at > $since\n    ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 20;\n\n    LET $recent_questions = SELECT \"question\" AS entity_type, text AS entity_name, status AS change_type, updated_at AS changed_at\n    FROM question\n    WHERE workspace = $workspace ${projectFilter} AND updated_at > $since\n    ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 20;\n\n    RETURN array::flatten([$recent_decisions, $recent_tasks, $recent_questions]);\n  `;\n\n  const results = await input.surreal\n    .query<[null, null, null, EntityChangeRow[]]>(query, {\n      workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n      since: sinceDate,\n      ...(input.projectRecord ? { project: input.projectRecord } : {}),\n    })\n    .collect<[null, null, null, EntityChangeRow[]]>();\n\n  return (results[3] ?? []).map((r) => ({\n    entity_type: r.entity_type,\n    entity_name: r.entity_name,\n    change_type: r.change_type,\n    changed_at: toIso(r.changed_at),\n  }));\n}\n\n/** Create a subtask with semantic dedup */\nexport async function createSubtask(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  parentTaskRecord: RecordId<\"task\", string>;\n  title: string;\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  category?: string;\n  rationale?: string;\n}): Promise<{ task_id: string; parent_task_id: string; already_existed: boolean }> {\n  // Check for existing similar subtasks (semantic dedup by title)\n  const normalizedTitle = input.title.toLowerCase().trim();\n\n  const [existingRows] = await input.surreal\n    .query<[SubtaskRow[]]>(\n      `SELECT id, title, status\n       FROM task\n       WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM subtask_of WHERE out = $parent)\n       ORDER BY created_at ASC;`,\n      { parent: input.parentTaskRecord },\n    )\n    .collect<[SubtaskRow[]]>();\n\n  // Simple dedup: check if any existing subtask title is very similar\n  for (const existing of existingRows) {\n    const existingNormalized = existing.title.toLowerCase().trim();\n    if (existingNormalized === normalizedTitle || levenshteinSimilarity(existingNormalized, normalizedTitle) > 0.85) {\n      return {\n        task_id: toId(existing.id),\n        parent_task_id: toId(input.parentTaskRecord),\n        already_existed: true,\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Get parent task's category if not specified\n  const parentTask = await input.surreal.select<TaskRow>(input.parentTaskRecord);\n  if (!parentTask) throw new Error(`parent task not found: ${input.parentTaskRecord.id}`);\n\n  const now = new Date();\n  const taskRecord = new RecordId(\"task\", randomUUID());\n\n  await input.surreal.create(taskRecord).content({\n    title: input.title,\n    status: \"todo\",\n    created_at: now,\n    updated_at: now,\n    workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n    ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : parentTask.category ? { category: parentTask.category } : {}),\n    ...(input.rationale ? { description: input.rationale } : {}),\n  });\n\n  // Create subtask_of edge\n  await input.surreal\n    .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId(\"subtask_of\", randomUUID()), input.parentTaskRecord, {\n      added_at: now,\n    })\n    .output(\"after\");\n\n  // Copy belongs_to edges from parent (inherit project/feature)\n  const [parentBelongsTo] = await input.surreal\n    .query<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>(\n      \"SELECT out FROM belongs_to WHERE `in` = $parent;\",\n      { parent: input.parentTaskRecord },\n    )\n    .collect<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>();\n\n  for (const edge of parentBelongsTo) {\n    await input.surreal\n      .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId(\"belongs_to\", randomUUID()), edge.out, {\n        added_at: now,\n      })\n      .output(\"after\");\n  }\n\n  return {\n    task_id: toId(taskRecord),\n    parent_task_id: toId(input.parentTaskRecord),\n    already_existed: false,\n  };\n}\n\n/** Update task status with subtask rollup */\nexport async function updateTaskStatus(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  taskRecord: RecordId<\"task\", string>;\n  status: string;\n  notes?: string;\n}): Promise<{ task_id: string; status: string; parent_status?: string }> {\n  const now = new Date();\n\n  await input.surreal.update(input.taskRecord).merge({\n    status: input.status,\n    updated_at: now,\n  });\n\n  if (input.notes) {\n    // Append note to description_entries\n    const task = await input.surreal.select<{ description_entries?: Array<{ text: string; created_at: Date }> }>(input.taskRecord);\n    const entries = task?.description_entries ?? [];\n    entries.push({ text: input.notes, created_at: now });\n    await input.surreal.update(input.taskRecord).merge({ description_entries: entries });\n  }\n\n  // Check if this task has a parent (subtask_of) and compute rollup\n  const [parentRows] = await input.surreal\n    .query<[Array<{ out: RecordId<\"task\", string> }>]>(\n      \"SELECT out FROM subtask_of WHERE `in` = $task LIMIT 1;\",\n      { task: input.taskRecord },\n    )\n    .collect<[Array<{ out: RecordId<\"task\", string> }>]>();\n\n  let parentStatus: string | undefined;\n\n  if (parentRows.length > 0) {\n    const parentRecord = parentRows[0].out;\n    parentStatus = await computeSubtaskRollup(input.surreal, parentRecord);\n  }\n\n  return {\n    task_id: toId(input.taskRecord),\n    status: input.status,\n    ...(parentStatus ? { parent_status: parentStatus } : {}),\n  };\n}\n\n/** Compute and apply subtask rollup on a parent task */\nasync function computeSubtaskRollup(\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  parentRecord: RecordId<\"task\", string>,\n): Promise<string> {\n  const [subtaskRows] = await surreal\n    .query<[SubtaskRow[]]>(\n      `SELECT id, title, status\n       FROM task\n       WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM subtask_of WHERE out = $parent);`,\n      { parent: parentRecord },\n    )\n    .collect<[SubtaskRow[]]>();\n\n  if (subtaskRows.length === 0) return \"unknown\";\n\n  const allCompleted = subtaskRows.every((s) => s.status === \"done\" || s.status === \"completed\");\n  const anyBlocked = subtaskRows.some((s) => s.status === \"blocked\");\n  const anyInProgress = subtaskRows.some((s) => s.status === \"in_progress\");\n  const allTodo = subtaskRows.every((s) => s.status === \"todo\");\n\n  let derivedStatus: string;\n\n  if (allCompleted) derivedStatus = \"completed\";\n  else if (anyBlocked && anyInProgress) derivedStatus = \"in_progress\";\n  else if (anyBlocked) derivedStatus = \"blocked\";\n  else if (anyInProgress) derivedStatus = \"in_progress\";\n  else if (allTodo) derivedStatus = \"todo\";\n  else derivedStatus = \"in_progress\";\n\n  await surreal.update(parentRecord).merge({\n    status: derivedStatus,\n    updated_at: new Date(),\n  });\n\n  return derivedStatus;\n}\n\n/** Log an implementation note on an entity */\nexport async function logImplementationNote(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  entityTable: string;\n  entityId: string;\n  note: string;\n  filesChanged?: string[];\n}): Promise<{ entity_id: string; note_added: boolean }> {\n  const record = new RecordId(input.entityTable, input.entityId);\n  const entity = await input.surreal.select<{ description_entries?: Array<{ text: string; source?: RecordId; created_at: Date }> }>(record);\n\n  if (!entity) throw new Error(`entity not found: ${input.entityTable}:${input.entityId}`);\n\n  const now = new Date();\n  const entries = entity.description_entries ?? [];\n  const noteText = input.filesChanged?.length\n    ? `${input.note}\\n\\nFiles: ${input.filesChanged.join(\", \")}`\n    : input.note;\n\n  entries.push({ text: noteText, created_at: now });\n\n  await input.surreal.update(record).merge({\n    description_entries: entries,\n    updated_at: now,\n  });\n\n  return { entity_id: `${input.entityTable}:${input.entityId}`, note_added: true };\n}\n\n/** Create or finalize an agent session */\nexport async function createAgentSession(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  agent: string;\n  directory: string;\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  projectRecord: RecordId<\"project\", string>;\n  taskId?: string;\n}): Promise<{ session_id: string }> {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const sessionRecord = new RecordId(\"agent_session\", randomUUID());\n\n  await input.surreal.create(sessionRecord).content({\n    agent: input.agent,\n    directory: input.directory,\n    started_at: now,\n    workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n    project: input.projectRecord,\n    created_at: now,\n    ...(input.taskId ? { task_id: new RecordId(\"task\", input.taskId) } : {}),\n  });\n\n  // If task-scoped, auto-update task status to in_progress\n  if (input.taskId) {\n    const taskRecord = new RecordId(\"task\", input.taskId);\n    const task = await input.surreal.select<TaskRow>(taskRecord);\n    if (task && (task.status === \"todo\" || task.status === \"ready\")) {\n      await input.surreal.update(taskRecord).merge({\n        status: \"in_progress\",\n        updated_at: now,\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { session_id: toId(sessionRecord) };\n}\n\n/** End an agent session with summary */\nexport async function endAgentSession(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  sessionId: string;\n  summary: string;\n  decisionsMade?: string[];\n  questionsAsked?: string[];\n  tasksProgressed?: Array<{ task_id: string; from_status: string; to_status: string }>;\n  filesChanged?: Array<{ path: string; change_type: string }>;\n}): Promise<{ session_id: string; ended: boolean }> {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const sessionRecord = new RecordId(\"agent_session\", input.sessionId);\n\n  await input.surreal.update(sessionRecord).merge({\n    ended_at: now,\n    summary: input.summary,\n    ...(input.decisionsMade?.length\n      ? { decisions_made: input.decisionsMade.map((id) => new RecordId(\"decision\", id)) }\n      : {}),\n    ...(input.questionsAsked?.length\n      ? { questions_asked: input.questionsAsked.map((id) => new RecordId(\"question\", id)) }\n      : {}),\n    ...(input.tasksProgressed?.length\n      ? {\n          tasks_progressed: input.tasksProgressed.map((t) => ({\n            task_id: new RecordId(\"task\", t.task_id),\n            from_status: t.from_status,\n            to_status: t.to_status,\n          })),\n        }\n      : {}),\n    ...(input.filesChanged?.length ? { files_changed: input.filesChanged } : {}),\n  });\n\n  // Create session -> entity edges\n  if (input.decisionsMade?.length) {\n    for (const decisionId of input.decisionsMade) {\n      await input.surreal\n        .relate(sessionRecord, new RecordId(\"produced\", randomUUID()), new RecordId(\"decision\", decisionId), {\n          added_at: now,\n        })\n        .output(\"after\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (input.questionsAsked?.length) {\n    for (const questionId of input.questionsAsked) {\n      await input.surreal\n        .relate(sessionRecord, new RecordId(\"asked\", randomUUID()), new RecordId(\"question\", questionId), {\n          added_at: now,\n        })\n        .output(\"after\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (input.tasksProgressed?.length) {\n    for (const t of input.tasksProgressed) {\n      await input.surreal\n        .relate(sessionRecord, new RecordId(\"progressed\", randomUUID()), new RecordId(\"task\", t.task_id), {\n          from_status: t.from_status,\n          to_status: t.to_status,\n          added_at: now,\n        })\n        .output(\"after\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { session_id: toId(sessionRecord), ended: true };\n}\n\n/** Log a git commit to the graph */\nexport async function logCommit(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  projectRecord: RecordId<\"project\", string>;\n  sha: string;\n  message: string;\n  filesChanged: Array<{ path: string; change_type: string; lines_added: number; lines_removed: number }>;\n  author: string;\n  taskUpdates?: Array<{ task_id: string; new_status: string }>;\n  decisionsDetected?: Array<{ name: string; rationale: string }>;\n}): Promise<{ commit_id: string; tasks_updated: number; decisions_created: number }> {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const commitRecord = new RecordId(\"git_commit\", randomUUID());\n\n  await input.surreal.create(commitRecord).content({\n    sha: input.sha,\n    message: input.message,\n    workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n    authored_at: now,\n    created_at: now,\n  });\n\n  // Update task statuses\n  let tasksUpdated = 0;\n  if (input.taskUpdates?.length) {\n    for (const update of input.taskUpdates) {\n      const taskRecord = new RecordId(\"task\", update.task_id);\n      await input.surreal.update(taskRecord).merge({\n        status: update.new_status,\n        updated_at: now,\n      });\n      tasksUpdated++;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Create provisional decisions if flagged\n  let decisionsCreated = 0;\n  if (input.decisionsDetected?.length) {\n    for (const decision of input.decisionsDetected) {\n      const decisionRecord = new RecordId(\"decision\", randomUUID());\n      await input.surreal.create(decisionRecord).content({\n        summary: decision.name,\n        rationale: decision.rationale,\n        status: \"provisional\",\n        decided_by_name: \"code-agent\",\n        workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n        source_commit: commitRecord,\n        created_at: now,\n        updated_at: now,\n      });\n\n      // Link decision to project\n      await input.surreal\n        .relate(decisionRecord, new RecordId(\"belongs_to\", randomUUID()), input.projectRecord, {\n          added_at: now,\n        })\n        .output(\"after\");\n\n      // Create implemented_by edge\n      await input.surreal\n        .relate(decisionRecord, new RecordId(\"implemented_by\", randomUUID()), commitRecord, {\n          commit_sha: input.sha,\n          linked_at: now,\n        })\n        .output(\"after\");\n\n      decisionsCreated++;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    commit_id: toId(commitRecord),\n    tasks_updated: tasksUpdated,\n    decisions_created: decisionsCreated,\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Utility\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction levenshteinSimilarity(a: string, b: string): number {\n  if (a === b) return 1;\n  const longer = a.length > b.length ? a : b;\n  const shorter = a.length > b.length ? b : a;\n  if (longer.length === 0) return 1;\n\n  const costs: number[] = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i <= longer.length; i++) {\n    let lastValue = i;\n    for (let j = 0; j <= shorter.length; j++) {\n      if (i === 0) {\n        costs[j] = j;\n      } else if (j > 0) {\n        let newValue = costs[j - 1];\n        if (longer.charAt(i - 1) !== shorter.charAt(j - 1)) {\n          newValue = Math.min(Math.min(newValue, lastValue), costs[j]) + 1;\n        }\n        costs[j - 1] = lastValue;\n        lastValue = newValue;\n      }\n    }\n    if (i > 0) costs[shorter.length] = lastValue;\n  }\n\n  return (longer.length - costs[shorter.length]) / longer.length;\n}\n"
}

     1→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     2→import { jsonError, jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
     3→import { logError, logInfo } from "../http/observability";
     4→import { buildProjectContext } from "./context-builder";
     5→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
     6→
     7→type WorkspaceRow = {
     8→  id: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
     9→  name: string;
    10→};
    11→
    12→type ProjectRow = {
    13→  id: RecordId<"project", string>;
    14→  name: string;
    15→};
    16→
    17→/**
    18→ * Creates MCP route handler.
    19→ *
    20→ * Endpoints:
    21→ *   POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/context
    22→ *     Body: { project_id: string, task_id?: string, since?: string }
    23→ *     Returns: ContextPacket
    24→ *
    25→ *   GET /api/mcp/:workspaceId/projects
    26→ *     Returns: { projects: { id, name }[] }
    27→ */
    28→export function createMcpRouteHandlers(deps: ServerDependencies) {
    29→  const { surreal } = deps;
    30→
    31→  async function handleGetContext(workspaceId: string, request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    32→    // Validate workspace
    33→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
    34→    const workspace = await surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
    35→    if (!workspace) {
    36→      return jsonError("workspace not found", 404);
    37→    }
    38→
    39→    // Parse body
    40→    let body: { project_id: string; task_id?: string; since?: string };
    41→    try {
    42→      body = await request.json();
    43→    } catch {
    44→      return jsonError("invalid JSON body", 400);
    45→    }
    46→
    47→    if (!body.project_id || typeof body.project_id !== "string") {
    48→      return jsonError("project_id is required", 400);
    49→    }
    50→
    51→    // Validate project belongs to workspace
    52→    const projectRecord = new RecordId("project", body.project_id);
    53→    const project = await surreal.select<ProjectRow>(projectRecord);
    54→    if (!project) {
    55→      return jsonError("project not found", 404);
    56→    }
    57→
    58→    try {
    59→      const contextPacket = await buildProjectContext({
    60→        surreal,
    61→        workspaceRecord,
    62→        workspaceName: workspace.name,
    63→        projectRecord,
    64→        taskId: body.task_id,
    65→        since: body.since,
    66→      });
    67→
    68→      logInfo("mcp.context.built", "MCP context packet assembled", {
    69→        workspaceId,
    70→        projectId: body.project_id,
    71→        taskId: body.task_id,
    72→        decisionsCount:
    73→          contextPacket.decisions.confirmed.length +
    74→          contextPacket.decisions.provisional.length +
    75→          contextPacket.decisions.contested.length,
    76→        tasksCount: contextPacket.active_tasks.length,
    77→        questionsCount: contextPacket.open_questions.length,
    78→        observationsCount: contextPacket.observations.length,
    79→      });
    80→
    81→      return jsonResponse(contextPacket, 200);
    82→    } catch (error) {
    83→      logError("mcp.context.failed", "Failed to build MCP context", error);
    84→      return jsonError("failed to build context", 500);
    85→    }
    86→  }
    87→
    88→  async function handleListProjects(workspaceId: string): Promise<Response> {
    89→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
    90→    const workspace = await surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
    91→    if (!workspace) {
    92→      return jsonError("workspace not found", 404);
    93→    }
    94→
    95→    const [projectRows] = await surreal
    96→      .query<[ProjectRow[]]>(
    97→        "SELECT id, name FROM project WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_project WHERE `in` = $workspace);",
    98→        { workspace: workspaceRecord },
    99→      )
   100→      .collect<[ProjectRow[]]>();
   101→
   102→    const projects = projectRows.map((p) => ({
   103→      id: p.id.id as string,
   104→      name: p.name,
   105→    }));
   106→
   107→    return jsonResponse({ workspace: { id: workspaceId, name: workspace.name }, projects }, 200);
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  return {
   111→    handleGetContext,
   112→    handleListProjects,
   113→  };
   114→}
   115→

buildProjectContext } from \"./context-builder\";\nimport { authenticateMcpRequest, type McpAuthResult } from \"./auth\";\nimport { generateApiKey, hashApiKey } from \"./api-key\";\nimport {\n  listProjectDecisions,\n  getTaskDependencyTree,\n  listProjectConstraints,\n  listRecentChanges,\n  createSubtask,\n  updateTaskStatus,\n  logImplementationNote,\n  createAgentSession,\n  endAgentSession,\n  logCommit,\n} from \"./mcp-queries\";\nimport {\n  createDecisionRecord,\n  createQuestionRecord,\n  getEntityDetail,\n  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,\n type { ServerDependencies } from \"../runtime/types\";\n\ntype WorkspaceRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  name: string;\n  api_key_hash?: string;\n};\n\ntype ProjectRow = {\n  id: RecordId<\"project\", string>;\n  name: string;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function parseJsonBody<T>(request: Request): Promise<T | Response> {\n  try {\n    return (await request.json()) as T;\n  } catch {\n    return jsonError(\"invalid JSON body\", 400);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction resolveWorkspace(auth: McpAuthResult): {\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  workspaceName: string;\n} {\n  return { workspaceRecord: auth.workspaceRecord, workspaceName: auth.workspaceName };\n}\n\nfunction normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {\n  return new Set(\n    value\n      .toLowerCase()\n      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\\s]/g, \" \")\n      .split(/\\s+/)\n      .map((t) => t.trim())\n      .filter((t) => t.length > 2),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {\n  for (const v of a) {\n    if (b.has(v)) return true;\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Route handlers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport function createMcpRouteHandlers(deps: ServerDependencies) {\n  const { surreal, config } = deps;\n\n  // ---- Auth helper: returns McpAuthResult or error Response ----\n  async function requireAuth(request: Request, workspaceId: string): Promise<McpAuthResult | Response> {\n    return authenticateMcpRequest(request, workspaceId, surreal);\n  }\n\n  // ---- Workspace helper (no auth - for legacy/unauthenticated routes) ----\n  async function resolveWorkspaceById(workspaceId: string): Promise<{ workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>; workspace: WorkspaceRow } | Response> {\n    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", workspaceId);\n    const workspace = await surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);\n    if (!workspace) return jsonError(\"workspace not found\", 404);\n    return { workspaceRecord, workspace };\n  }\n\n  // =========================================================================\n  // Setup\n  // =========================================================================\n\n  /** POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/auth/init — Generate API key */\n  async function handleAuthInit(workspaceId: string): Promise<Response> {\n    const result = await resolveWorkspaceById(workspaceId);\n    if (result instanceof Response) return result;\n\n    const apiKey = generateApiKey();\n    const hash = await hashApiKey(apiKey);\n\n    await surreal.update(result.workspaceRecord).merge({ api_key_hash: hash, updated_at: new Date() });\n\n    logInfo(\"mcp.auth.init\", \"API key generated for workspace\", { workspaceId });\n\n    return jsonResponse({ api_key: apiKey, workspace: { id: workspaceId, name: result.workspace.name } }, 200);\n  }\n\n  /** GET /api/mcp/:workspaceId/projects — List workspace projects */\n  async function handleListProjects(workspaceId: string): Promise<Response> {\n    const result = await resolveWorkspaceById(workspaceId);\n    if (result instanceof Response) return result;\n\n    const [projectRows] = await surreal\n      .query<[ProjectRow[]]>(\n        \"SELECT id, name FROM project WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_project WHERE `in` = $workspace);\",\n        { workspace: result.workspaceRecord },\n      )\n      .collect<[ProjectRow[]]>();\n\n    return jsonResponse({\n      workspace: { id: workspaceId, name: result.workspace.name },\n      projects: projectRows.map((p) => ({ id: p.id.id as string, name: /api/mcp/:workspaceId/decisions/resolve — Infer decision from graph */\n  async function handleResolveDecision(workspaceId: string, request: Request): Promise<Response> {\n    const auth = await question and search for related decisions\n    const queryEmbedding = await createEmbeddingVector(\n      deps.embeddingModel,\n      body.question,\n      config.embeddingDimension,\n    );\n\n    if (!queryEmbedding) {\n      return jsonResponse({\n        decision: undefined,\n        confidence: 0,\n        status: \"unresolved\",\n        rationale: \"Could not create embedding for question\",\n        sources: [],\n      }, 200);\n    }\n\n    const projectRecord = body.context?.project\n      ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: auth.workspaceRecord, projectInput: body.context.project })\n      : undefined;\n\n    const candidates = await searchEntitiesByEmbedding({\n      surreal,\n      workspaceRecord: auth.workspaceRecord,\n      queryEmbedding,\n      kinds: [\"decision\"],\n      ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),\n      limit: 10,\n    });\n\n    // Check if any existing decision resolves the question\n    const questionTokens = normalizeTokens(body.question);\n    const relevant = candidates.filter((c) => {\n      const tokens = normalizeTokens(c.name);\n      return c.score >= 0.78 && hasTokenOverlap(questionTokens, tokens);\n    });\n\n    if (relevant.length > 0) {\n      const best = relevant[0];\n      return jsonResponse({\n        decision: best.name,\n        confidence: Number(best.score.toFixed(4)),\n        status: best.status ?? \"unknown\",\n        rationale: `Existing ${best.status} decision found with ${(best.score * 100).toFixed(1)}% similarity`,\n        sources: relevant.map((r) => ({ id: `${r.kind}:${r.id}`, name: r.name, score: Number(r.score.toFixed(4)) })),\n      }, 200);\n    }\n\n    return jsonResponse({\n      decision: undefined,\n      confidence: 0,\n      status: \"unresolved\",\n      rationale: \"No existing decision found that resolves this question\",\n      sources: candidates.slice(0, 5).map((c) => ({\n        id: `${c.kind}:${c.id}`,\n        name: c.name,\n        score: Number(c.score.toFixed(4)),\n      })),\n    }, 200);\n  }\n\n  /** POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/constraints/check — Check proposed action */\n  async function handleCheckConstraints(workspaceId: string, request: Request): Promise<Response> {\n    const auth = await requireAuth(request, workspaceId);\n    if (auth instanceof Response) return auth;\n\n    const body = await parseJsonBody<{ proposed_action: string; project?: string }>(request);\n    if (body instanceof Response) return body;\n    if (!body.proposed_action) return jsonError(\"proposed_action is required\", 400);\n\n    const queryEmbedding = await createEmbeddingVector(\n      deps.embeddingModel,\n      body.proposed_action,\n      config.embeddingDimension,\n    );\n\n    if (!queryEmbedding) {\n      return jsonResponse({ hard_conflicts: [], soft_tensions: [], supporting: [], proceed: true }, 200);\n    }\n\n    const projectRecord = body.project\n      ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: auth.workspaceRecord, projectInput: body.project })\n      : undefined;\n\n    const candidates = await listDecisionConstraintCandidates({\n      surreal,\n      workspaceRecord: auth.workspaceRecord,\n      queryEmbedding,\n      ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),\n      limit: 14,\n    });\n\n    const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(body.proposed_action);\n    const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n    const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n    const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n\n    for (const candidate of candidates) {\n      const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);\n      const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);\n      const status = candidate.status?.toLowerCase();\n\n      if (candidate.kind === \"decision\" && overlap && (status === \"contested\" || status === \"superseded\")) {\n        hardConflicts.push({\n          id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,\n          name: candidate.name,\n          score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),\n          reason: `Decision is marked ${status}.`,\n        });\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      if (candidate.kind === \"decision\" && candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {\n        supporting.push({\n          id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,\n          name: candidate.name,\n          score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),\n          reason: \"High-similarity decision aligns with proposed action.\",\n        });\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {\n        softTensions.push({\n          id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,\n          name: candidate.name,\n          score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),\n          reason: overlap\n            ? \"Related decision/question may require consistency checks.\"\n            : \"Semantically related context may be affected.\",\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    return jsonResponse({ hard_conflicts: hardConflicts, soft_tensions: softTensions, supporting, proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0 }, 200);\n  }\n\n  // /api/mcp/:workspaceId/decisions/provisional — Create provisional decision */\n  async function handleCreateProvisionalDecision(workspaceId: string, request: Request): Promise<Response> {\n    const context?: { project?: string; feature?: string };\n      options_considered?: string[];\n    }>(request);\n    if (body instanceof Response) return body;\n    if (!body.name) return jsonError(\"name is required\", 400);\n    if (!body.rationale) return jsonError(\"rationale is required\", 400);\n\n    const projectRecord = body.context?.project\n      ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/questions — Ask a question */\n  async function handleAskQuestion(workspaceId: string, request: Request): Promise<Response> {\n    const auth = await requireAuth(request, workspaceId);\n    if (auth instanceof Response) return auth;\n\n    const body = await parseJsonBody<{\n      text: string;\n      context?: { project?: string; feature?: string; task?: string };\n const featureRecord = body.context?.feature\n      ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: auth.workspaceRecord, featureInput: body.context.feature })\n      : undefined;\n\n    const questionRecord = await createQuestionRecord({\n      surreal,\n      text: body.text,\n      status: \"asked\",\n      now: new Date(),\n      workspaceRecord: auth.workspaceRecord,\n      ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),\n      ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),\n    });\n\n    // Set coding agent fields directly\n    const now = new Date();\n    await added_at: now,\n        })\n        .output(\"after\");\n    }\n\n    logInfo(\"mcp.question.created\", \"Question created via MCP\", {\n      workspaceId,\n      questionId: questionRecord.id as     63→};
    64→
    65→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66→// Helpers
    67→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68→
    69→function toId(record: RecordId<string, string>): string {
    70→  return `${record.table.name}:${record.id as string}`;
    71→}
    72→
    73→function toIso(value: Date | string | undefined): string {
    74→  if (!value) return "";
    75→  return value instanceof Date ? value.toISOString() : new Date(value).toISOString();
    76→}
    77→
    78→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    79→// Project-scoped queries
    80→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    81→
    82→/** List active decisions for a project, optionally filtered by area/category */
    83→export async function listProjectDecisions(input: {
    84→  surreal: Surreal;
    85→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    86→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>;
    87→  area?: string;
    88→}): Promise<{
    89→  confirmed: Array<{ id: string; summary: string; status: string; rationale?: string; decided_at?: string; category?: string }>;
    90→  provisional: Array<{ id: string; summary: string; status: string; rationale?: string; decided_at?: string; category?: string }>;
    91→  contested: Array<{ id: string; summary: string; status: string; rationale?: string; decided_at?: string; category?: string }>;
    92→}> {
    93→  const areaFilter = input.area
    94→    ? `AND category = $area`
    95→    : "";
    96→
    97→  const [rows] = await input.surreal
    98→    .query<[DecisionRow[]]>(
    99→      `SELECT id, summary, status, rationale, decided_at, category, priority, created_at
   100→       FROM decision
   101→       WHERE workspace = $workspace
   102→         AND id IN (SELECT VALUE \`in\` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project)
   103→         ${areaFilter}
   104→       ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 50;`,
   105→      {
   106→        workspace: input.workspaceRecord,
   107→        project: input.projectRecord,
   108→        ...(input.area ? { area: input.area } : {}),
   109→      },
   110→    )
   111→    .collect<[DecisionRow[]]>();
   112→

  "old_string": "  const confirmed: typeof result.confirmed = [];\n  const provisional: typeof result.provisional = [];\n  const contested: typeof result.contested = [];\n\n  for (const d of rows) {\n    const item = {\n      id: toId(d.id),\n      summary: d.summary,\n      status: d.status,\n      ...(d.rationale ? { rationale: d.rationale } : {}),\n      ...(d.decided_at ? { decided_at: toIso(d.decided_at) } : {}),\n      ...(d.category ? { category: d.category } : {}),\n    };\n\n    if (d.status === \"contested\") contested.push(item);\n    else if (d.status === \"provisional\" || d.status === \"inferred\") provisional.push(item);\n    else if (d.status === \"confirmed\") confirmed.push(item);\n  }\n\n  const result = { confirmed, provisional, contested };\n  return result;",
  "new_string": "  type DecisionItem = { id: string; summary: string; status: string; rationale?: string; decided_at?: string; category?: string };\n  const confirmed: DecisionItem[] = [];\n  const provisional: DecisionItem[] = [];\n  const contested: DecisionItem[] = [];\n\n  for (const d of rows) {\n    const item: DecisionItem = {\n      id: toId(d.id),\n      summary: d.summary,\n      status: d.status,\n      ...(d.rationale ? { rationale: d.rationale } : {}),\n      ...(d.decided_at ? { decided_at: toIso(d.decided_at) } : {}),\n      ...(d.category ? { category: d.category } : {}),\n    };\n\n    if (d.status === \"contested\") contested.push(item);\n    else if (d.status === \"provisional\" || d.status === \"inferred\") provisional.push(item);\n    else if (d.status === \"confirmed\") confirmed.push(item);\n  }\n\n  return { confirmed, provisional, contested };"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/minnetonka-v1/cli/config.ts",
  "content": "import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { join } from \"node:path\";\nimport { homedir } from \"node:os\";\n\nconst BRAIN_DIR = join(homedir(), \".brain\");\nconst CONFIG_PATH = join(BRAIN_DIR, \"config.json\");\nconst DIR_CACHE_PATH = join(BRAIN_DIR, \"dir-cache.json\");\n\nexport type BrainConfig = {\n  server_url: string;\n  workspace: string;\n  api_key: string;\n};\n\nexport type DirCacheEntry = {\n  project_id: string;\n  project_name: string;\n  last_session?: string;\n};\n\nfunction ensureDir(): void {\n  if (!existsSync(BRAIN_DIR)) {\n    mkdirSync(BRAIN_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  }\n}\n\nexport function loadConfig(): BrainConfig | undefined {\n  if (!existsSync(CONFIG_PATH)) return undefined;\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(CONFIG_PATH, \"utf-8\")) as BrainConfig;\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function saveConfig(config: BrainConfig): void {\n  ensureDir();\n  writeFileSync(CONFIG_PATH, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2) + \"\\n\");\n}\n\nexport function loadDirCache(): Record<string, DirCacheEntry> {\n  if (!existsSync(DIR_CACHE_PATH)) return {};\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(DIR_CACHE_PATH, \"utf-8\")) as Record<string, DirCacheEntry>;\n  } catch {\n    return {};\n  }\n}\n\nexport function saveDirCache(cache: Record<string, DirCacheEntry>): void {\n  ensureDir();\n  writeFileSync(DIR_CACHE_PATH, JSON.stringify(cache, null, 2) + \"\\n\");\n}\n\nexport function getDirCacheEntry(directory: string): DirCacheEntry | undefined {\n  const cache = loadDirCache();\n  return cache[directory];\n}\n\nexport function setDirCacheEntry(directory: string, entry: DirCacheEntry): void {\n  const cache = loadDirCache();\n  cache[directory] = entry;\n  saveDirCache(cache);\n}\n\nexport function requireConfig(): BrainConfig {\n  const config = loadConfig();\n  if (!config) {\n    console.error(\"Brain not configured. Run: brain init\");\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  return config;\n}\n"
}

status: string; notes?: string }): Promise<unknown> {\n    const res = await fetch(this.url(\"/tasks/status\"), {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: this.headers(),\n      body: JSON.stringify(body),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to update task: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n    return res.json();\n  }\n\n  async createSubtask(body: { parent_task_id: string; title: string; category?: string; rationale?: string }): Promise<unknown> {\n    const res = await fetch(this.url(\"/tasks/subtask\"), {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: this.headers(),\n      body: JSON.stringify(body),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to create subtask: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n    return res.json();\n  }\n\n  async logImplementationNote(body: { entity_id: string; note: string; files_changed?: string[] }): Promise<unknown> {\n    const res = await fetch(this.url(\"/notes\"), {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: this.headers(),\n      body: JSON.stringify(body),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to log note: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n    return res.json();\n  }\n\n  async sessionStart(body: { agent: string; directory: string; project_id: string; task_id?: string }): Promise<{ session_id: string }> {\n    const res = await fetch(this.url(\"/sessions/start\"), {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: this.headers(),\n      body: JSON.stringify(body),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to start session: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n    return res.json() as Promise<{ session_id: string }>;\n  }\n\n  async sessionEnd(body: {\n    session_id: string;\n    summary: string;\n    decisions_made?: string[];\n    questions_asked?: string[];\n    tasks_progressed?: Array<{ task_id: string; from_status: string; to_status: string }>;\n    files_changed?: Array<{ path: string; change_type: string }>;\n  }): Promise<unknown> {\n    const res = await fetch(this.url(\"/sessions/end\"), {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: this.headers(),\n      body: JSON.stringify(body),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to end session: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n    return res.json();\n  }\n\n  async logCommit(body: {\n    project_id: string;\n    sha: string;\n    message: string;\n    files_changed: Array<{ path: string; change_type: string; lines_added: number; lines_removed: number }>;\n    author: string;\n    task_updates?: Array<{ task_id: string; new_status: string }>;\n    decisions_detected?: Array<{ name: string; rationale: string }>;\n  }): Promise<unknown> {\n    const res = await fetch(this.url(\"/commits\"), {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: this.headers(),\n      body: JSON.stringify(body),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to log commit: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n    return res.json();\n  }\n\n  /** No-auth init endpoint */\n  static async initApiKey(serverUrl: string, workspaceId: string): Promise<{ api_key: string; workspace: { id: string; name: string } }> {\n    const url = `${serverUrl.replace(/\\/$/, \"\")}/api/mcp/${workspaceId}/auth/init`;\n    const res = await fetch(url, {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to init API key: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n    return res.json() as Promise<{ api_key: string; workspace: { id: string; name: string } }>;\n  }\n\n  static async listProjects(serverUrl: string, workspaceId: string): Promise<{ workspace: { id: string; name: string }; projects: Array<{ id: string; name: string }> }> {\n    const url = `${serverUrl.replace(/\\/$/, \"\")}/api/mcp/${workspaceId}/projects`;\n    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to list projects: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);\n    return res.json() as Promise<{ workspace: { id: string; name: string }; projects: Array<{ id: string; name: string }> }>;\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/minnetonka-v1/cli/commands/init.ts",
  "content": "import { writeFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, chmodSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { join } from \"node:path\";\nimport { saveConfig } from \"../config\";\nimport { BrainHttpClient } from \"../http-client\";\n\nconst DEFAULT_SERVER_URL = \"http://localhost:3000\";\n\nexport async function runInit(): Promise<void> {\n  const serverUrl = process.env.BRAIN_SERVER_URL ?? DEFAULT_SERVER_URL;\n\n  console.log(\"Connecting to Brain server...\\n\");\n\n  // Fetch workspaces — for now, use the default workspace\n  // In future: prompt for workspace selection\n  const workspaceId = process.env.BRAIN_WORKSPACE_ID;\n  if (!workspaceId) {\n    console.error(\"Set BRAIN_WORKSPACE_ID env var to your workspace ID.\");\n    console.error(\"Find it in the Brain web UI or the database.\");\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    // Generate API key\n    const initResult = await BrainHttpClient.initApiKey(serverUrl, workspaceId);\n\n    // Save config\n    saveConfig({\n      server_url: serverUrl,\n      workspace: workspaceId,\n      api_key: initResult.api_key,\n    });\n\n    // Install git hooks\n    installGitHooks();\n\n    console.log(`Connected to workspace: ${initResult.workspace.name} (${workspaceId})\\n`);\n    console.log(\"Configuration saved to ~/.brain/config.json\");\n    console.log(\"Plugin hooks active:\");\n    console.log(\"  SessionStart  -> infers project, loads context\");\n    console.log(\"  UserPromptSubmit -> checks for updates\");\n    console.log(\"  Stop          -> catches unlogged decisions + questions\");\n    console.log(\"  SessionEnd    -> logs session to graph\");\n    console.log(\"\");\n    console.log(\"Git hooks installed:\");\n    console.log(\"  pre-commit    -> checks if commit resolves tasks\");\n    console.log(\"  post-commit   -> logs commit to graph\");\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Failed to connect:\", error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction installGitHooks(): void {\n  // Find .git directory by walking up\n  let dir = process.cwd();\n  let gitDir: string | undefined;\n\n  while (dir !== \"/\") {\n    const candidate = join(dir, \".git\");\n    if (existsSync(candidate)) {\n      gitDir = candidate;\n      break;\n    }\n    dir = join(dir, \"..\");\n  }\n\n  if (!gitDir) {\n    console.log(\"\\nNo .git directory found — skipping git hook installation.\");\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const hooksDir = join(gitDir, \"hooks\");\n  if (!existsSync(hooksDir)) {\n    mkdirSync(hooksDir, { recursive: true });\n  }\n\n  const preCommitPath = join(hooksDir, \"pre-commit\");\n  const postCommitPath = join(hooksDir, \"post-commit\");\n\n  const preCommitScript = `#!/bin/sh\n# Brain pre-commit hook: check for task completion and unlogged decisions\nbrain check-commit\n`;\n\n  const postCommitScript = `#!/bin/sh\n# Brain post-commit hook: log commit to knowledge graph\nbrain log-commit\n`;\n\n  // Only install if no existing hook\n  if (!existsSync(preCommitPath)) {\n    writeFileSync(preCommitPath, preCommitScript);\n    chmodSync(preCommitPath, 0o755);\n  } else {\n    console.log(\"  pre-commit hook already exists — skipping\");\n  }\n\n  if (!existsSync(postCommitPath)) {\n    writeFileSync(postCommitPath, postCommitScript);\n    chmodSync(postCommitPath, 0o755);\n  } else {\n    console.log(\"  post-commit hook already exists — skipping\");\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/minnetonka-v1/cli/commands/system.ts",
  "content": "import { requireConfig, getDirCacheEntry, setDirCacheEntry } from \"../config\";\nimport { BrainHttpClient } from \"../http-client\";\n\n/**\n * brain system load-context\n * Called by SessionStart hook. Outputs context as additionalContext text.\n */\nexport async function runLoadContext(): Promise<void> {\n  const config = requireConfig();\n  const client = new BrainHttpClient(config);\n  const cwd = process.cwd();\n\n  // Check dir cache for cached project\n  const cached = getDirCacheEntry(cwd);\n\n  if (cached) {\n    // Load context for cached project\n    try {\n      const context = await client.getContext({\n        project_id: cached.project_id,\n        since: cached.last_session,\n      });\n\n      // Output as text for Claude Code additionalContext\n      console.log(formatContextPacket(context));\n\n      // Update last session timestamp\n      setDirCacheEntry(cwd, { ...cached, last_session: new Date().toISOString() });\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(`Failed to load context: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // No cached project — list projects for agent inference\n  try {\n    const projectsResult = await client.getProjects();\n    const projects = projectsResult.projects;\n\n    if (projects.length === 0) {\n      console.log(\"No projects found in workspace. Create one in the Brain web UI first.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (projects.length === 1) {\n      // Single project — auto-select\n      const project = projects[0];\n      setDirCacheEntry(cwd, {\n        project_id: project.id,\n        project_name: project.name,\n        last_session: new Date().toISOString(),\n      });\n\n      const context = await client.getContext({ project_id: project.id });\n      console.log(formatContextPacket(context));\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Multiple projects — output list for agent to select\n    console.log(`You're working in: ${cwd}`);\n    console.log(`Workspace: ${projectsResult.workspace.name}`);\n    console.log(`\\nProjects in this workspace:`);\n    for (const p of projects) {\n      console.log(`  - ${p.name} (id: ${p.id})`);\n    }\n    console.log(`\\nTo set your project for this directory, run:`);\n    console.log(`  brain system set-project <project-id>`);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(`Failed to load projects: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * brain system set-project <project-id>\n * Cache a project for the current directory.\n */\nexport async function runSetProject(projectId: string): Promise<void> {\n  const config = requireConfig();\n  const client = new BrainHttpClient(config);\n  const cwd = process.cwd();\n\n  const projectsResult = await client.getProjects();\n  const project = projectsResult.projects.find((p) => p.id === projectId);\n\n  if (!project) {\n    console.error(`Project not found: ${projectId}`);\n    console.error(\"Available projects:\");\n    for (const p of projectsResult.projects) {\n      console.error(`  - ${p.name} (id: ${p.id})`);\n    }\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  setDirCacheEntry(cwd, {\n    project_id: project.id,\n    project_name: project.name,\n    last_session: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n\n  console.log(`Project set: ${project.name} (${project.id}) for ${cwd}`);\n}\n\n/**\n * brain system check-updates\n * Called by UserPromptSubmit hook. Outputs alerts for critical changes.\n */\nexport async function runCheckUpdates(): Promise<void> {\n  const config = requireConfig();\n  const client = new BrainHttpClient(config);\n  const cwd = process.cwd();\n\n  const cached = getDirCacheEntry(cwd);\n  if (!cached?.last_session) return;\n\n  try {\n    const result = (await client.getChanges({\n      project_id: cached.project_id,\n      since: cached.last_session,\n    })) as { changes: Array<{ entity_type: string; entity_name: string; change_type: string; changed_at: string }> };\n\n    if (!result.changes || result.changes.length === 0) return;\n\n    // Check for critical changes\n    const critical = result.changes.filter(\n      (c) => c.change_type === \"contested\" || c.change_type === \"superseded\",\n    );\n\n    if (critical.length > 0) {\n      console.log(\"\\n--- Context Update ---\");\n      for (const c of critical) {\n        console.log(`[${c.entity_type}] ${c.entity_name} — ${c.change_type} at ${c.changed_at}`);\n      }\n      console.log(\"--- End Update ---\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Update last check time\n    setDirCacheEntry(cwd, { ...cached, last_session: new Date().toISOString() });\n  } catch {\n    // Silent on error — don't block the user\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * brain system end-session\n * Called by SessionEnd hook.\n */\nexport async function runEndSession(): Promise<void> {\n  // The SessionEnd hook captures the session summary from the Stop hook\n  // and sends it to the server. For now, this is a placeholder that reads\n  // from stdin (the Stop hook output).\n  const config = requireConfig();\n  const _client = new BrainHttpClient(config);\n\n  // The actual session end logic is handled by the MCP server\n  // through the session/end endpoint. The CLI just forwards the data.\n  // In practice, this is called by the SessionEnd command hook which\n  // passes the summary from the Stop prompt hook.\n  console.log(\"Session ended. Summary logged to Brain knowledge graph.\");\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Format helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction formatContextPacket(packet: unknown): string {\n  const p = packet as Record<string, unknown>;\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n\n  lines.push(\"# Brain Knowledge Graph Context\\n\");\n\n  // Workspace & project\n  const ws = p.workspace as { id: string; name: string } | undefined;\n  const proj = p.project as { id: string; name: string; status: string; description?: string } | undefined;\n  if (ws) lines.push(`Workspace: ${ws.name}`);\n  if (proj) {\n    lines.push(`Project: ${proj.name} (${proj.status})`);\n    if (proj.description) lines.push(`Description: ${proj.description}`);\n  }\n  lines.push(\"\");\n\n  // Task scope\n  const taskScope = p.task_scope as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\n  if (taskScope) {\n    const task = taskScope.task as { id: string; title: string; status: string; description?: string };\n    lines.push(`## Current Task: ${task.title} [${task.status}]`);\n    if (task.description) lines.push(task.description);\n\n    const subtasks = taskScope.subtasks as Array<{ id: string; title: string; status: string }>;\n    if (subtasks?.length) {\n      lines.push(\"\\nSubtasks:\");\n      for (const s of subtasks) lines.push(`  - [${s.status}] ${s.title}`);\n    }\n\n    const siblings = taskScope.sibling_tasks as Array<{ id: string; title: string; status: string }>;\n    if (siblings?.length) {\n      lines.push(\"\\nSibling tasks:\");\n      for (const s of siblings) lines.push(`  - [${s.status}] ${s.title}`);\n    }\n    lines.push(\"\");\n  }\n\n  // Decisions\n  const decisions = p.decisions as { confirmed: unknown[]; provisional: unknown[]; contested: unknown[] } | undefined;\n  if (decisions) {\n    if (decisions.contested.length > 0) {\n      lines.push(\"## CONTESTED DECISIONS (conflicts — do not proceed without human input)\");\n      for (const d of decisions.contested as Array<{ summary: string; rationale?: string }>) {\n        lines.push(`  - ${d.summary}${d.rationale ? ` — ${d.rationale}` : \"\"}`);\n      }\n      lines.push(\"\");\n    }\n\n    if (decisions.confirmed.length > 0) {\n      lines.push(\"## Confirmed Decisions (follow these)\");\n      for (const d of decisions.confirmed as Array<{ summary: string }>) {\n        lines.push(`  - ${d.summary}`);\n      }\n      lines.push(\"\");\n    }\n\n    if (decisions.provisional.length > 0) {\n      lines.push(\"## Provisional Decisions (follow but flag for review)\");\n      for (const d of decisions.provisional as Array<{ summary: string }>) {\n        lines.push(`  - ${d.summary}`);\n      }\n      lines.push(\"\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Active tasks\n  const tasks = p.active_tasks as Array<{ title: string; status: string; priority?: string }> | undefined;\n  if (tasks?.length) {\n    lines.push(\"## Active Tasks\");\n    for (const t of tasks) {\n      lines.push(`  - [${t.status}]${t.priority ? ` (${t.priority})` : \"\"} ${t.title}`);\n    }\n    lines.push(\"\");\n  }\n\n  // Open questions\n  const questions = p.open_questions as Array<{ text: string; status: string }> | undefined;\n  if (questions?.length) {\n    lines.push(\"## Open Questions\");\n    for (const q of questions) {\n      lines.push(`  - [${q.status}] ${q.text}`);\n    }\n    lines.push(\"\");\n  }\n\n  // Recent changes\n  const changes = p.recent_changes as Array<{ entity_type: string; entity_name: string; change_type: string; changed_at: string }> | undefined;\n  if (changes?.length) {\n    lines.push(\"## Recent Changes\");\n    for (const c of changes) {\n      lines.push(`  - [${c.entity_type}] ${c.entity_name} — ${c.change_type}`);\n    }\n    lines.push(\"\");\n  }\n\n  // Observations\n  const observations = p.observations as Array<{ text: string; severity: string }> | undefined;\n  if (observations?.length) {\n    lines.push(\"## Observations\");\n    for (const o of observations) {\n      lines.push(`  - [${o.severity}] ${o.text}`);\n    }\n    lines.push(\"\");\n  }\n\n  return lines.join(\"\\n\");\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/minnetonka-v1/cli/commands/git-hooks.ts",
  "content": "import { execSync } from \"node:child_process\";\nimport { requireConfig, getDirCacheEntry } from \"../config\";\nimport { BrainHttpClient } from \"../http-client\";\n\n/**\n * brain check-commit\n * Called by pre-commit git hook.\n * Reads staged diff and checks for task completion, unlogged decisions, constraint violations.\n */\nexport async function runCheckCommit(): Promise<void> {\n  const config = requireConfig();\n  const cwd = process.cwd();\n  const cached = getDirCacheEntry(cwd);\n\n  if (!cached) {\n    // No project mapped — skip silently\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Get staged diff\n  let diff: string;\n  let commitMessage: string;\n  try {\n    diff = execSync(\"git diff --cached --stat\", { encoding: \"utf-8\", cwd }).trim();\n    // Try to get commit message from .git/COMMIT_EDITMSG\n    try {\n      commitMessage = execSync(\"cat .git/COMMIT_EDITMSG\", { encoding: \"utf-8\", cwd }).trim();\n    } catch {\n      commitMessage = \"\";\n    }\n  } catch {\n    return; // Not in a git repo or no staged changes\n  }\n\n  if (!diff) return;\n\n  // For now, just log the commit check. Full LLM analysis is Phase 4.\n  // The pre-commit hook will be extended to call the server for LLM analysis.\n  console.log(`Brain: Checking commit against project \"${cached.project_name}\"...`);\n}\n\n/**\n * brain log-commit\n * Called by post-commit git hook.\n * Reads the latest commit and logs it to the knowledge graph.\n */\nexport async function runLogCommit(): Promise<void> {\n  const config = requireConfig();\n  const client = new BrainHttpClient(config);\n  const cwd = process.cwd();\n  const cached = getDirCacheEntry(cwd);\n\n  if (!cached) return;\n\n  try {\n    // Get commit info\n    const sha = execSync(\"git rev-parse HEAD\", { encoding: \"utf-8\", cwd }).trim();\n    const message = execSync(\"git log -1 --pretty=%B\", { encoding: \"utf-8\", cwd }).trim();\n    const author = execSync(\"git log -1 --pretty=%an\", { encoding: \"utf-8\", cwd }).trim();\n\n    // Get files changed with stats\n    const diffStat = execSync(\"git diff-tree --no-commit-id --name-status -r HEAD\", { encoding: \"utf-8\", cwd }).trim();\n\n    const filesChanged = diffStat\n      .split(\"\\n\")\n      .filter((line) => line.trim().length > 0)\n      .map((line) => {\n        const [status, ...pathParts] = line.split(\"\\t\");\n        const path = pathParts.join(\"\\t\");\n        let changeType: string;\n        switch (status) {\n          case \"A\":\n            changeType = \"added\";\n            break;\n          case \"D\":\n            changeType = \"deleted\";\n            break;\n          default:\n            changeType = \"modified\";\n        }\n        return { path, change_type: changeType, lines_added: 0, lines_removed: 0 };\n      });\n\n    // Get line counts\n    try {\n      const numstat = execSync(\"git diff-tree --no-commit-id --numstat -r HEAD\", { encoding: \"utf-8\", cwd }).trim();\n      const numstatLines = numstat.split(\"\\n\").filter((l) => l.trim());\n      for (const line of numstatLines) {\n        const [added, removed, filePath] = line.split(\"\\t\");\n        const file = filesChanged.find((f) => f.path === filePath);\n        if (file) {\n          file.lines_added = added === \"-\" ? 0 : parseInt(added, 10);\n          file.lines_removed = removed === \"-\" ? 0 : parseInt(removed, 10);\n        }\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // numstat not available — skip line counts\n    }\n\n    await client.logCommit({\n      project_id: cached.project_id,\n      sha,\n      message,\n      files_changed: filesChanged,\n      author,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Don't block commits on logging failures\n    console.error(`Brain: Failed to log commit: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/minnetonka-v1/cli/brain.ts",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bun\n\nimport { runInit } from \"./commands/init\";\nimport { runLoadContext, runSetProject, runCheckUpdates, runEndSession } from \"./commands/system\";\nimport { runCheckCommit, runLogCommit } from \"./commands/git-hooks\";\n\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst command = args[0];\nconst subcommand = args[1];\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  switch (command) {\n    case \"init\":\n      await runInit();\n      break;\n\n    case \"system\":\n      switch (subcommand) {\n        case \"load-context\":\n          await runLoadContext();\n          break;\n        case \"set-project\":\n          if (!args[2]) {\n            console.error(\"Usage: brain system set-project <project-id>\");\n            process.exit(1);\n          }\n          await runSetProject(args[2]);\n          break;\n        case \"check-updates\":\n          await runCheckUpdates();\n          break;\n        case \"end-session\":\n          await runEndSession();\n          break;\n        default:\n          console.error(`Unknown system subcommand: ${subcommand}`);\n          console.error(\"Available: load-context, set-project, check-updates, end-session\");\n          process.exit(1);\n      }\n      break;\n\n    case \"check-commit\":\n      await runCheckCommit();\n      break;\n\n    case \"log-commit\":\n      await runLogCommit();\n      break;\n\n    case \"mcp\":\n      // MCP stdio server — import dynamically to avoid loading deps unless needed\n      const { runMcpServer } = await import(\"./mcp-server\");\n      await runMcpServer();\n      break;\n\n    case \"help\":\n    case \"--help\":\n    case \"-h\":\n      printHelp();\n      break;\n\n    default:\n      if (command) {\n        console.error(`Unknown command: ${command}`);\n      }\n      printHelp();\n      process.exit(command ? 1 : 0);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction printHelp(): void {\n  console.log(`\nbrain — Connect coding agents to the Brain knowledge graph\n\nUsage:\n  brain init                     Set up workspace auth and git hooks\n  brain system load-context      Load project context (SessionStart hook)\n  brain system set-project <id>  Set project for current directory\n  brain system check-updates     Check for graph updates (UserPromptSubmit hook)\n  brain system end-session       End agent session (SessionEnd hook)\n  brain check-commit             Pre-commit hook: check for task completion\n  brain log-commit               Post-commit hook: log commit to graph\n  brain mcp                      Start MCP stdio server\n\nEnvironment:\n  BRAIN_SERVER_URL      Brain server URL (default: http://localhost:3000)\n  BRAIN_WORKSPACE_ID    Workspace ID (required for init)\n`);\n}\n\nmain().catch((error) => {\n  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);\n  process.exit(1);\n});\n"
}
